Mr. Speaker, to my esteemed colleague in the House of Representatives,

**[00:03:45.940] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000010)  
I once again stand to sponsor the budget of an agency that is vital to the strength of our economy across various sectors,

**[00:03:55.780] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000011)  
such as public procurement, telecommunications,

**[00:04:00.000] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000012)

**[00:04:02.740] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000013)

**[00:04:09.940] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000014)

**[00:04:16.320] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000015)

**[00:04:22.880] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000016)

**[00:04:30.000] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000017)

**[00:04:45.940] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000019)

**[00:04:51.360] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000020)

**[00:05:00.000] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000022)

**[00:05:08.440] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000024)

**[00:05:14.740] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000025)

**[00:05:29.360] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000029)

**[00:05:34.060] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000030)

**[00:05:39.060] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000031)

**[00:05:45.560] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000032)

**[00:05:50.200] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000033)

for fiscal year 2026 to date.

**[00:07:07.900] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000048)  
Across sectors such as energy and financial services,

**[00:07:11.840] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000049)

**[00:07:19.460] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000051)

**[00:07:22.600] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000052)  
which aims to foster competition among

**[00:07:29.740] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000054)

**[00:07:32.900] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000055)  
Lastly, given the importance of awareness and competition laws

**[00:07:36.940] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000056)  
by the private sector and its regulators,

**[00:07:40.900] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000057)

**[00:07:46.280] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000058)

**[00:07:55.760] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000060)

**[00:08:11.900] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000061)  
With a budget of P587.6 million, the PCC was able to achieve the aforementioned initiatives, as I believe that the PCC can do more with more resources to help the government protect public funds.

**[00:08:28.620] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000062)  
While the overall national budget for 2027 sees a 6% increase, our specific request for the PCC is calibrated to address the complex challenges,

**[00:08:41.900] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000063)

**[00:08:45.540] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000064)

**[00:08:54.040] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000065)

**[00:09:10.830] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000067)

**[00:09:24.780] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000070)

**[00:09:36.840] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000071)  
I urge my colleagues to support this budget.

**[00:10:24.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000082)

**[00:10:33.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000084)  
Is it true that the PCC had already conducted a competition impact assessment?

**[00:10:40.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000085)  
of the Green Energy Auction Program, or GEAP, with the DOE and ERC?

**[00:10:48.320] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000086)  
Yes, the PCC conducted the study in 2025 and then conducted policy dialogue with the DOE and ERC last January and March of this year.

**[00:10:59.520] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000087)  
The study examined whether specific features of the program may create or facilitate anti-competitive effects.

**[00:11:10.360] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000088)  
in the renewable energy sector, and identified policy options to address potential competition concerns,

**[00:11:18.620] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000089)  
including the role of the PCC in coordination with the DOE, ERC, and other relevant bodies.

**[00:11:26.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000090)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor.

**[00:11:30.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000091)

**[00:11:36.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000092)  
involving the geographic segmentation of the green energy sector?

**[00:11:40.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000094)  
the determination of Green Energy Auction Reserve, or GEAR, prices and the disclosure of price caps and conduct of pre-bid conferences, among others.

**[00:11:54.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000095)  
Can you share those competition concerns for the benefit of the body and the sector as a whole?

**[00:12:02.740] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000096)  
The PCC's role is to primarily assess the competition effects of GEAP

**[00:12:10.260] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000097)  
within the existing framework and recommend pro-competitive reforms to the agencies concerned,

**[00:12:16.480] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000098)  
namely DOE and ERC.

**[00:12:19.000] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000099)  
The study identified three potential competition concerns.

**[00:12:23.040] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000100)  
First, the location-specific or per-grid auction design.

**[00:12:28.740] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000101)  
Second is the consistency and transparency of GEAR price determination

**[00:12:35.060] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000102)  
and the disclosure of auction-related information, particularly GEAR prices,

**[00:12:40.240] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000104)  
and the conduct

**[00:12:42.400] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000105)  
of pre-bid conferences.

**[00:12:44.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000106)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker,

**[00:12:45.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000107)  
Distinguished Sponsor.

**[00:12:47.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000108)

**[00:14:56.740] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000171)

**[00:15:30.000] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000175)

**[00:15:41.060] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000176)

**[00:15:48.520] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000177)

**[00:15:57.060] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000178)

**[00:16:00.000] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000179)

**[00:16:16.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000181)

**[00:16:20.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000182)  
Mr. Speaker, Good Sponsor, a few days ago, I have raised urgent concerns related to privacy risks in digital platforms.

**[00:16:30.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000183)  
Incidentally, I came across the information that PCC's market study on social media and search platform found that the Philippines' digital platform market is highly concentrated and identified the accumulation of user data as a potential source of market power.

**[00:16:51.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000184)  
It specifically discussed Facebook and Google and observed that data advantages,

**[00:16:58.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000185)

**[00:17:06.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000186)  
What does the PCC intend to do with these findings beyond publishing the market study?

**[00:17:14.700] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000187)  
The PCC has recommended incorporating data privacy considerations more explicitly into competition assessments.

**[00:17:22.840] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000188)  
Studying data, portability, and interoperability.

**[00:17:28.840] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000189)  
Strengthening coordination with privacy regulators and considering legislative measures to update the competition framework for data-driven markets.

**[00:17:41.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000190)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[00:17:44.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000191)  
In 2024, the PCC received substantial resources for the capacity building to foster competition projects,

**[00:17:54.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000192)  
but only 34% of the continuing...

**[00:17:58.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000193)  
appropriation for the foreign-assisted project was obligated.

**[00:18:02.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000194)  
Is that correct?

**[00:18:06.360] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000195)  
Yes.

**[00:18:07.840] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000196)  
We note that said amount is part of the continuing appropriation for the project released in 2023.

**[00:18:13.540] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000197)  
At that time, PCC requested for the issuance of SARO amounting 104 million only.

**[00:18:21.380] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000198)  
Equivalent to the loan proceeds downloaded by ADB, however,

**[00:18:25.480] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000199)  
DBM issued 280 million instead.

**[00:18:28.840] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000200)  
Which resulted to the excess resources.

**[00:18:31.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000201)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:18:36.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000202)  
ADB's project data showed that as of June 23, 2026,

**[00:18:42.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000203)  
cumulative contract awards were only 45% and cumulative loan disbursements were 41%

**[00:18:50.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000204)  
and the loan has now been extended to June 30, 2028.

**[00:18:54.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000205)  
Those figures are not identical to COA's

**[00:18:58.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000206)  
fiscal year 2024 utilization ratios

**[00:19:03.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000207)  
but they still indicate that the substantial portion of the project financing remained undisbursed.

**[00:19:12.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000208)  
Before Congress provides additional appropriation,

**[00:19:14.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000209)  
can the PCC give us its quarterly obligation and disbursement target for fiscal year 2027

**[00:19:23.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000210)  
and identify exactly what activities will absorb these funds.

**[00:19:28.840] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000211)  
The PCC can point to the loan extension, revised procurement, programming, additional project proposals, and strengthened monitoring as measures intended to accelerate implementation.

**[00:19:46.210] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000212)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker. And for my last question, how many pending antitrust or pending cases have been filed or are being reviewed by the Philippine Competition Commission?

**[00:20:20.800] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000213)  
There are seven active cases ongoing, Your Honor.

**[00:20:24.950] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000213-split-2)  
Can we divulge what those seven are, or will it be against the PCC's confidentiality clause?

**[00:20:37.930] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000213-split-3)  
We will submit the complete data to you, Your Honor, for you to review.

**[00:20:43.870] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000214)  
Okay. Thank you. Because, Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor, you know the Philippine Competition Commission plays a big role, especially to those merging companies, no, para hindi ho tayo maiwanan sa ere at walang collusion, especially po pagdating sa fuel. As you know, malaki ang problema natin sa fuel ngayon, and I hope the PCC will take cognizance when it comes to energy sources. Again,

**[00:21:12.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000215)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Speaker, and I always [unclear] for your budget for PCC for this year. Thank you very much.

**[00:21:24.220] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000216)  
Thank you, Honorable De Jesus, Majority Leader.

**[00:21:28.840] Rep. Rhea Mae A. Gullas** (asr-000217)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Robert Nazal for his interpellation.

**[00:21:34.800] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000218)  
The Honorable Robert Nazal of Partylist BH is recognized. Please proceed.

**[00:21:39.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000219)  
Good evening, Mr. Chair.

**[00:21:42.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000220)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, may I ask the PCC, have you studied what happens when a major retailer is both the gatekeeper of the market and a competitor of suppliers selling inside its stores?

**[00:22:01.420] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000221)  
Yes.

**[00:22:05.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000223)  
Pinag-aaralan niyo po yan.

**[00:22:07.840] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000224)  
Yes.

**[00:22:08.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000225)  
Major retailers carry products made by independent suppliers.

**[00:22:12.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000226)  
But they may also develop their own private label products.

**[00:22:18.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000227)  
Sometimes products very similar to those already being successfully sold by their suppliers.

**[00:22:25.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000228)  
So there is nothing inherently illegal about private labels.

**[00:22:28.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000229)  
But the retailer knows what sells, controls the shelf, and counter space, controls inventory and promotions,

**[00:22:42.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000230)  
and then competes against the supplier with its own product.

**[00:22:47.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000231)  
Nangyayari po ito.

**[00:22:50.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000232)  
At what point does this become a competition concern?

**[00:22:54.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000233)  
Or when does it become?

**[00:22:56.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000234)  
Inaanalyze po ba natin when it becomes predatory in practice?

**[00:23:24.010] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000240)  
The PCC is closely monitoring that's happening and a thorough investigation will be conducted.

**[00:23:32.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000241)  
Meron na po ba kayong nakitang mga ganito? And were there complaints, for example, that were filed with the PCC that was actually investigated upon?

**[00:23:42.680] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000242)  
There were minor complaints.

**[00:23:44.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000243)  
And what intervention, Mr. Chair and distinguished colleague, ang ginawa po ng PCC?

**[00:23:52.340] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000244)  
There were minor complaints but it was given an attention and it was informed to the manufacturer.

**[00:24:04.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000245)  
So, meron po ba tayong portal na pwedeng magreklamo ang, let's say, SMEs?

**[00:24:10.680] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000246)  
We can use the website.

**[00:24:12.680] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000247)  
Of the PCC and the social media.

**[00:24:16.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000248)  
And is it very easy to file a complaint? And once an SME, for example, files a complaint against any predatory practice, ganong katagal ang response time?

**[00:24:35.100] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000249)  
Yes, pwedeng madali ang mag-file ng complaint kasi pwede sa online at sa Facebook page ng PCC.

**[00:24:42.680] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000250)  
Kaya ganon, kadali ang pag-file, may investigation kagad.

**[00:24:47.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000251)  
Mr. Chair, distinguished colleague, let me give a practical example.

**[00:24:52.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000252)  
Kung may independent brand na successful, then the retailer comes out with a very similar own brand product at a lower price.

**[00:25:02.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000253)  
Tapos yung sariling brand niya, prominently displayed and readily available sa counter.

**[00:25:07.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000254)  
While yung competing product, wala doon, kailangan magbayad.

**[00:25:12.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000255)  
Tapos yung staff, kailangan pang umalas para kunin.

**[00:25:16.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000256)  
The retailer controls both the competition and the playing field.

**[00:25:20.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000257)  
Does the PCC examine practices like this?

**[00:25:27.300] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000258)  
Yes, yes.

**[00:25:31.760] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000259)  
That's called self-preferencing.

**[00:25:36.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000260)  
And ano pong actions ang pwede natin gawin in case meron tayong nahuhuling ganito?

**[00:25:42.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000261)  
What intervention can the PCC do in behalf of those experiencing predatory practices?

**[00:25:52.500] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000262)  
The PCC can file a case against the retailer.

**[00:26:00.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000263)  
What if the retailer po is a giant?

**[00:26:11.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000264)  
Malakas, ha?

**[00:26:12.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000265)  
And pwede po niyang balikan kasi yung halimbawa, pwede niyang balikan yung SME.

**[00:26:19.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000266)  
And if there are only a few retailers in the country, paano po ang mangyayari kung ang gagawin niya,

**[00:26:28.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000267)  
ang magiging balik niya doon sa nagreklamo, ay ibabanan niya sa shelf?

**[00:26:33.780] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000268)  
The PCC is mandated. May mandato ang PCC na kahit malaking tao man lang, kahit gaano kalaki, pwedeng sagasaan.

**[00:26:41.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000269)  
And the PCC?

**[00:26:42.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000270)  
The PCC guarantees that it can protect small players against practices such as this.

**[00:26:48.440] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000271)  
Definitely.

**[00:26:48.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000272)  
Thank you for that, Mr. Chair, distinguished sponsor.

**[00:26:54.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000273)  
Mr. Chair, has PCC ever conducted a market study comparing how major retailers treat their own private label products versus competing independent brands?

**[00:27:07.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000274)  
In shelf and counter placement, inventory availability,

**[00:27:11.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000275)  
promotion,

**[00:27:12.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000276)  
access to sales data,

**[00:27:15.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000277)  
pricing,

**[00:27:16.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000278)  
supplier fees,

**[00:27:17.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000279)  
payment terms,

**[00:27:18.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000280)  
and other conditions.

**[00:27:20.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000281)  
Because ang concern po natin is not simply that they copied the product or made something cheaper.

**[00:27:26.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000282)  
The concern is whether a powerful retailer can identify a successful product from one of its suppliers,

**[00:27:34.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000283)  
introduce its own competing version,

**[00:27:36.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000284)  
and then use its control of the retail channel to give its own product.

**[00:27:42.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000285)  
An unfair advantage, and eventually squeeze the independent supplier out.

**[00:27:48.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000286)  
Does our present competition framework adequately protect against that?

**[00:27:53.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000287)  
Thank you very much.

**[00:27:57.240] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000288)  
Wala pang public study, but we had compelled a very big retailer to be fair.

**[00:28:10.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000289)  
So ano pong kailangan na?

**[00:28:12.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000290)  
Is there anything you need, for example, from Congress in terms of amending your charter, strengthening it?

**[00:28:19.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000291)  
Meron po ba tayo naiisip na mga pwede natin baguhin para po palakasin at bigyan ng pangil ang Philippine Competition Commission

**[00:28:29.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000292)  
para po lalo natin maprotektahan ang ating mga maliliit na nag-uumpisang maghanap buhay?

**[00:28:39.180] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000297)  
There is a pending bill on the committee.

**[00:28:42.200] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000298)  
On economic affairs, I think.

**[00:28:46.180] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000299)  
And the PCC is closely working with the committee.

**[00:28:51.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000300)  
I would like to co-author that, Mr. Chair, and the distinguished sponsor.

**[00:28:56.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000301)  
Mr. Chair, is PCC looking at the same gatekeeper problem in online platforms

**[00:29:03.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000302)  
where the operator of the marketplace may also sell or promote products that compete with merchants

**[00:29:09.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000303)  
dependent on that marketplace?

**[00:29:12.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000304)  
What safeguards does PCC consider necessary when the platform or retailer controls both

**[00:29:19.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000305)  
access to customer and the competitive environment?

**[00:29:22.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000306)  
Para sa online naman po ito, distinguished sponsor.

**[00:29:29.200] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000307)  
Yes, we are looking to it.

**[00:29:34.200] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000308)  
And in terms of safeguarding the…

**[00:29:51.970] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000318)  
[unclear]

**[00:30:02.350] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000323)  
We are closely monitoring the market, and if there is a violation, we are bound to file a complaint.

**[00:30:18.010] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000324)  
Kaya ako po tinatanong ito, Distinguished Sponsor, Mr. Speaker. Kasi po, ang napansin ko, noon, lalo na nung pandemic, naging advantage po ngayon yung online retailers.

**[00:30:34.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000325)  
So yung online platforms were helping yung ating maliliit na negosyante. Parang ang lumalabas ngayon, yung ating mga retailers, giant retailers,

**[00:30:46.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000326)  
tsaka yung online at digital platforms are already charging, if not the same, yung ating digital platforms are charging even more.

**[00:30:57.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000327)  
And ang gusto ko po sanang alamin is kung natitignan po natin yung charges na sinisingil nila.

**[00:31:03.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000328)  
Kasi if you will ask and do conduct a study or maybe an online survey, tanungin nyo po yung mga nagbibenta online.

**[00:31:14.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000329)  
The digital platforms are now charging maybe up to 60%.

**[00:31:20.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000330)  
And parang ang nangyayari, kung 100 pesos yung binibenta mo, 60 pesos na yung kinukuha nila, minsan 70 pesos pa.

**[00:31:29.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000331)  
So ang matitira sa'yo, 30 pesos, kukunin mo pa dun yung cost mo.

**[00:31:35.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000332)  
So my concern is the PCC looking into that. Kasi po, dapat ho, I think it's about time that we monitor this.

**[00:31:43.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000333)  
Kasi kung ano-anong charges ang nilalagay nila.

**[00:31:49.240] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000334)  
We have looked into the digital platforms but not into that specific issue but we will see it now.

**[00:31:56.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000335)  
Thank you for that, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[00:31:58.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000336)  
Mr. Chair, Distinguished Sponsor, have you also studied the competition implications when a conglomerate owns both a bank and businesses in sectors heavily dependent on financing such as real estate?

**[00:32:13.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000337)  
Even if related party lending complies with banking regulations, can preferential access to financing reinforce market power in the conglomerate's non-banking business?

**[00:32:26.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000338)  
And has PCC studied this as a structural competition issue?

**[00:32:35.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000339)  
Ang tinatanong ko po ay yung possible whether PCC studies cross-sector competitive effects.

**[00:32:43.020] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000340)  
Yes, we have.

**[00:32:45.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000341)  
So you're already looking into that, Mr. Chair?

**[00:32:49.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000342)  
Mr. Distinguished Sponsor, the PCC?

**[00:32:51.540] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000343)  
Yes, we have.

**[00:32:53.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000344)  
So meron na po ba kayong nakita na, for example, industry na because of this, nagkakaroon ng unfair advantage?

**[00:33:00.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000345)  
And if this is so, what is the, ano po ang gagawin ng PCC in order to help level the playing field?

**[00:33:13.020] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000346)  
So far, wala pang nakikitang ganyan. Pero pag meron, rest assured na maaksyonan kagalit.

**[00:33:20.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000347)  
So baka po pwede natin tignan, maybe we can use AI to look into this kung ano po yung mga possible effects.

**[00:33:28.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000348)  
Kasi siyempre, magkakaroon po ng unfair advantage, di ba?

**[00:33:34.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000349)  
Mr. Chair, Mr. Chair, Distinguished Colleague, the PCC's proposed 2027 budget is approximately 832 million.

**[00:33:43.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000350)  
Including funding for competition, capacity building.

**[00:33:48.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000351)  
What concrete sector reviews and enforcement milestones will this funding deliver?

**[00:33:55.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000352)  
Particularly for retail, digital platforms, and other highly concentrated markets.

**[00:34:01.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000353)  
Will the PCC use its own investigative authority proactively rather than require small businesses to initiate every case?

**[00:34:10.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000354)  
So ang tinatanong po natin is,

**[00:34:13.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000355)  
mag-aantay pa po ba tayo ng mga complaint bago tayong makagalaw?

**[00:34:20.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000356)  
Or will we proactively seek measures which will protect small businesses?

**[00:34:37.860] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000357)  
We actively monitor the social medias.

**[00:34:43.020] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000358)  
And any complaint, and we will immediately act on this matter.

**[00:34:49.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000359)  
May I request for a plan?

**[00:34:52.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000360)  
Kung ano, how do you intend to spend the 832 million?

**[00:34:57.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000361)  
And ano po yung safeguards na pwede na, safeguards that you plan to impose?

**[00:35:07.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000362)  
Plan sector studies, measurable outputs.

**[00:35:11.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000363)  
And how SMEs?

**[00:35:13.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000364)  
And suppliers can safely provide market information without fear of commercial retaliation?

**[00:35:20.540] Rep. Dante S. Garcia** (asr-000365)  
Yes, Your Honor, we will provide you a copy.

**[00:35:28.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000366)  
So, I'm, that's all for all, para sa mga tanong ko.

**[00:35:34.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000367)  
Maraming salamat sa PCC.

**[00:35:35.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000368)  
And I hope the PCC will proactively craft,

**[00:35:43.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000369)  
proactively craft measures na makakatulong po at makakaprotekta po sa ating maliliit na naghahanap buhay.

**[00:35:51.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000370)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:35:53.620] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000371)  
Thank you, Honorable Nazal.

**[00:35:55.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000372)  
In behalf of the minority, I would like to terminate.

**[00:36:00.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000373)  
Tama ba?

**[00:36:03.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000375)  
I move.

**[00:36:04.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000376)  
I sorry, I move to terminate.

**[00:36:07.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000377)  
The period of sponsorship.

**[00:36:11.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000378)  
And amendment for the PCC.

**[00:36:13.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000379)  
PCC, for the, for the budget of the PCC.

**[00:36:17.660] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000380)  
What does the House say?

**[00:36:20.600] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000381)  
Mr. Speaker, on the part of the majority, we join the minority in the motion of terminate the period of interpellations on the proposed budget of the PCC.

**[00:36:34.560] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000383)  
There is a joint motion to terminate the interpellations on the proposed budget of the Philippine Competition Commission.

**[00:36:43.020] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000384)  
Is there any objection?

**[00:36:44.920] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000385)  
The chair hears none.

**[00:36:47.060] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000386)  
The interpellations on the proposed budget of the Philippine Competition Commission is hereby terminated.

**[00:36:55.660] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000387)  
Majority leader.

**[00:37:04.740] Rep. Daphne A. Lagon** (asr-000388)  
Mr. Speaker, I move to suspend session for a few minutes.

**[00:37:08.000] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000389)  
Session is suspended.

**[00:38:59.990] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000399)  
Session is resumed. Majority Leader.

**[00:39:04.500] Rep. Daphne A. Lagon** (asr-000399-split-2)  
Mr. Speaker, I move to consider the proposed budget of the Metropolitan Baguio City,

**[00:39:11.920] Rep. Daphne A. Lagon** (asr-000400)  
La Trinidad, Itogon, Sablan, Tuba and Tublay Development Authority.

**[00:39:19.200] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000400-split-2)  
Is there any objection? The Chair hears none. The motion is approved. Majority Leader.

**[00:39:24.300] Rep. Daphne A. Lagon** (asr-000400-split-3)  
Mr. Speaker, may we acknowledge the heads and officials of the Metropolitan Baguio City, Assistant Secretary Maria Monica C. Costales, and Director Mary Gail Antonia Kila.

**[00:39:43.020] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000401)  
To the family of the Metropolitan Baguio City, La Trinidad, Itogon, Sablan, Tuba, and Tublay Development Authority,

**[00:39:52.020] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000402)  
represented by the Honorable ASEC, Maria Monica C. Costales, Ph.D.,

**[00:39:57.840] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000403)  
welcome to the House of Representatives.

**[00:40:00.700] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000404)  
Majority Leader.

Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable

**[00:55:03.040] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000895)

**[00:55:07.430] Rep. Rhea Mae A. Gullas** (asr-000896)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[00:55:57.520] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000899)

**[00:55:59.640] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000900)  
Majority Leader.

**[00:56:01.020] Rep. Rhea Mae A. Gullas** (asr-000901)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Presley De Jesus for his interpellation.

**[00:56:09.580] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-000902)  
The Honorable Presley De Jesus of Partilis, Felrica is recognized. Please proceed.

**[00:56:14.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000903)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, good evening.

**[00:56:18.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000904)  
Earlier this year, DBM issued Corporate Budget Memorandum No. 48 for the Fiscal Year 2027 Corporate Budget Call.

**[00:56:28.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000905)  
Under the policy, it was stated that the DBM should not be involved in corporate budget calls.

**[00:56:31.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000906)  
Under the policy, it was stated that a GOCC receiving 60% or more its funding from national government subsidies for the past 10 consecutive years is automatically flagged for mandatory institutional review for GOCCs covered by Republic Act No. 10149.

**[00:56:51.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000907)  
That review is to be conducted by GCG. Is this correct, Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor?

**[00:56:59.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000908)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:57:01.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000909)  
And my follow-up question, then under those parameters, how many GOCCs under GCG's jurisdiction currently meet the 60% subsidy for 10 years threshold?

**[00:57:19.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000910)  
The study is ongoing regarding the number, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:57:22.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000911)  
Are we talking about 2026?

**[00:57:28.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000912)  
Or 2025, I mean? I'm sorry.

**[00:57:50.740] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000914)  
The GCG is just checking their records, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:58:01.462] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000916)  
...

**[00:58:31.020] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000917)  
The GCG will just submit the list of GOCCs that satisfy their requirement, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:58:44.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000918)  
That's okay with my distinguished colleague.

**[00:58:47.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000919)  
Fair enough, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:58:49.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000920)  
I gladly accept that.

**[00:58:51.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000921)  
My next question,

**[00:58:55.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000922)  
have institutional reviews which are now mandated already commenced?

**[00:59:01.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000923)  
For every one of those GOCCs.

**[00:59:03.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000924)  
If not, what the timetables are we looking at?

**[00:59:31.020] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000925)  
Regarding the monitoring and evaluation of GOCCs, Mr. Speaker, the GCG currently conducts three.

**[00:59:40.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000926)  
Yes, three.

**[00:59:42.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000927)  
There's the performance evaluation system for GOCCs, and then there's the performance evaluation for directors in the GOCC sector.

**[00:59:49.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000928)  
And then the GCG also produces corporate governance scorecards.

**[00:59:54.020] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000929)  
But as per the finalization of the reports for fiscal year 2020,

**[01:00:00.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000930)  
the GCG is dependent on the submission of COA financial statement.

**[01:00:07.220] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000931)  
So as of now, review is still currently ongoing, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:00:10.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000932)  
So do we expect that later this year or it's going to be next year?

**[01:00:16.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000933)  
Yes, later in this year, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:00:18.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000934)  
How many directors are, as you mentioned, that under review?

**[01:00:22.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000935)  
How many of them?

**[01:00:30.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000936)  
For, Mr. Speaker, for clarification, director, total directors for all the GOCCs that are being reviewed, total.

**[01:00:57.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000937)  
We're pulling up the actual number.

**[01:01:00.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000940)  
So estimated around, approximately around 500 directors, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:01:04.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000941)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:01:10.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000942)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, could the good sponsor provide the following information for the consideration of this body?

**[01:01:21.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000943)  
The amount of national government support received by these GOCCs during the past 10 years, its performance, scorecard results,

**[01:01:29.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000944)  
and the current status of GCG's institutional review.

**[01:01:36.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000945)  
It's important because the new DBM policy says they are now subject to mandatory institutional review to be conducted by GCG.

**[01:01:48.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000946)  
Kasi po, meron na pong DBM policy na bago ngayon.

**[01:01:53.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000947)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker, just to clarify, the first would be the amount of government support received by the GOCCs.

**[01:01:58.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000948)  
What's the second one, Mr. Speaker?

**[01:02:02.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000949)  
No, that's the question because I'm just interjecting that there's a new DBM policy.

**[01:02:09.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000950)  
Now, they have to be subjected for mandatory institutional review to be conducted by GCG.

**[01:02:16.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000951)  
Yes, sir. Government support.

**[01:02:21.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000952)  
Yes, sir. Government support.

**[01:02:38.220] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000954)  
Mr. Speaker, the GCG is just pulling up the actual number.

**[01:02:43.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000955)  
However, just an overall observation regarding the amount of government support,

**[01:02:48.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000956)  
it's been declining given that those GOCCs that previously needed government support are now improving in performance.

**[01:02:57.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000957)  
Thank you.

**[01:02:58.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000958)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:03:04.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000959)  
According to GCG's classification as of June 30, 2026,

**[01:03:11.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000960)  
there are 21 corporations classified under abolition,

**[01:03:17.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000961)  
meaning they are being considered for closure,

**[01:03:22.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000962)  
winding down or absorption into other government corporations.

**[01:03:26.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000963)  
GCG was subsequently reported as saying that the Commission hopes to complete the liquidation of at least five of them within 2026.

**[01:03:39.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000964)  
Is that still the target?

**[01:03:43.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000965)  
Yes.

**[01:03:44.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000966)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker, that is correct.

**[01:03:46.420] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000967)  
As of June 30, there are 21 GOCCs that have been abolished and under liquidation.

**[01:03:55.900] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000968)  
And there's also...

**[01:03:56.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000969)  
And there's also 11 that has been abolished and completed liquidation.

**[01:03:59.380] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000970)  
As for the number of GOCCs with terminal reports to be submitted to the Office of the President,

**[01:04:08.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000971)  
there are 11 for this year.

**[01:04:10.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000972)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[01:04:11.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000973)  
Yeah.

**[01:04:13.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000974)  
Mr. Speaker, this is your sponsor.

**[01:04:15.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000975)  
Without prejudice to the non-disclosure,

**[01:04:18.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000976)  
may din nyo bangbanggitin kung sino mo ito or it's going under review pa rin?

**[01:04:23.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000977)  
Or if not, you can submit to this...

**[01:04:26.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000978)  
to this body kung sino yung mga yon.

**[01:04:30.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000979)  
If you think it's going to be a violation of any rules or guidelines under GCG.

**[01:04:43.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000980)  
Oh, sorry.

**[01:04:45.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000981)  
I stand corrected, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:04:46.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000982)  
The number of abolished GOCCs pipeline for terminal report in 2026 is actually five.

**[01:04:54.380] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000983)  
Yeah.

**[01:04:54.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000984)  
But...

**[01:04:55.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000985)  
But...

**[01:04:55.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000986)  
That's why I was mentioned a while ago that there are still five.

**[01:04:59.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000987)  
That's why for my next question,

**[01:05:03.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000988)  
that means even five are completed this year.

**[01:05:07.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000989)  
A significant number will still remain under abolition status.

**[01:05:12.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000990)  
For how long has each of these 21 corporations been under abolition?

**[01:05:48.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001011)  
Um, Mr. Speaker, given the extensive process of settling the obligations and liabilities of those GOCCs

**[01:05:58.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001012)  
Those GOCCs that are non-performing, as well as the fulfillment of compromise agreements, it's a lengthy period. As early as 2013, there are still GOCCs that are being processed. So, roughly around 13 years.

**[01:06:21.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001013)  
Yeah, Mr. Speaker. For the information of everyone, GOCC stands for government-owned controlled corporation.

**[01:06:28.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001014)  
Am I right? And they're not asking subsidy or funding from our government, only GCG. Now, because my understanding, when you're a GOCC, you generate income. Am I right?

**[01:06:47.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001015)  
Yes.

**[01:06:48.060] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001016)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:06:49.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001017)  
So, how come those 5 or 21 you mentioned, Wilco, are winding down or for abolition? Ibig ho bang sabihin?

**[01:06:59.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001018)  
Nalulugi, hindi sila kumikita, or part of downsizing talaga?

**[01:07:05.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001019)  
Mr. Speaker, based on the charter of the GCG and all of its guidelines, there are six qualifications for the recommendation of this positive action.

**[01:07:20.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001020)  
One, the GOCC's function is no longer relevant to state or national development policy.

**[01:07:27.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001021)  
Second, it...

**[01:07:28.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001022)  
It's duplicate or unnecessarily overlaps with other government agencies.

**[01:07:33.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001023)  
The third one, the GOCC is not producing desired outcomes or is no longer achieving its objectives, as stated.

**[01:07:40.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001024)  
The fourth would be, the GOCC would be dormant or non-operational.

**[01:07:46.420] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001025)  
The fifth would be, the GOCC would be involved in activities that is determined to be best carried out by a private sector firm.

**[01:07:58.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001026)  
And the sixth one would be, the GOCC's nature of operations would require consolidation under a holding company.

**[01:08:05.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001027)  
So, those are the six qualifications for recommendation for abolition, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:08:09.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001028)  
The number five, or I think number five, you mentioned number four, that is going to be sold to private.

**[01:08:16.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001029)  
Is that what I understand?

**[01:08:18.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001030)  
Tama ba yun? Intindi ko?

**[01:08:20.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001031)  
Uh, there...

**[01:08:23.060] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001032)  
Okay.

**[01:08:26.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001033)  
Okay.

**[01:08:28.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001034)  
The fifth one, Mr. Speaker, the GCG assesses and sees if the activity in which the GOCC is involved would be best carried out by a private sector firm.

**[01:08:48.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001035)  
So, when you say best carried out by a private firm, what does it mean?

**[01:08:54.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001036)  
Are we going to sell it or just going to give it away?

**[01:08:57.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001037)  
Anong bang...

**[01:08:58.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001038)  
Ang tindi sa private firm will just be carried away.

**[01:09:04.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001039)  
It will be carried out.

**[01:09:17.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001040)  
Okay.

**[01:09:29.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001043)  
[unclear]

**[01:09:38.430] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001044)  
[unclear]

**[01:09:50.650] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001049)  
[unclear]

**[01:10:07.690] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001066)  
Mr. Speaker, regarding abolition,

**[01:10:11.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001067)  
if the

**[01:10:12.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001068)  
particular GOCC would be

**[01:10:14.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001069)  
recommended for abolition,

**[01:10:17.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001070)  
it's usually

**[01:10:18.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001071)  
the

**[01:10:19.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001072)  
employees, the assets,

**[01:10:22.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001073)  
and all the operations would be

**[01:10:23.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001074)  
absorbed by the government

**[01:10:26.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001075)  
agency, like a relevant government

**[01:10:27.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001076)  
agency, with related

**[01:10:29.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001077)  
operations to the GOCC for abolition.

**[01:10:32.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001078)  
Mr. Speaker.

434.805 million in incentives in 2024 was reviewed and approved by the Civil Service Commission in June 2024. Dahil sa CPCS 1 and CPCS 2, may mga benepisyong nawala o nabawasan sa mga manggagawa sa GFIs.

**[01:37:00.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001519)  
Sa provident fund, some were enjoying 40% employer share, pero dahil sa CPCS 1 and CPCS 2, 10% na lang. Iyan ang malaking problema. Madam Sponsor, let us establish a basic principle of law.

**[01:37:30.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001520)  
Ang Republic Act 10149, GOCC Governance Act, ay pinasa noong 2011, a general law for standardization of GOCCs. Fast forward to 2019, ipinasa ng Kongreso ang RA 11199, Social Security Act of 2018, isang specific law.

**[01:38:00.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001521)  
Section 3(d) gave the Social Security Commission power to fix reasonable compensation, allowances, and benefits of its personnel. Ngayon ginagamit ng GCG yung CPCS 2 na inilabas noong 2025

**[01:38:30.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001522)  
para i-override at tanggalin ang mga benepisyong ito. In constant communication ako sa union ng SSS. So alin ang mas mataas, Madam Sponsor, isang batas na pinasa ng Kongreso o isang executive order

**[01:39:00.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001523)  
na nanggaling sa Office of the President, Madam Sponsor, Mr. Speaker?

10149, Mr. Speaker.

10149, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:42:21.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001546)  
So, in statutory construction, the specific and later law prevails over a general and earlier law.

**[01:42:30.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001547)

**[01:42:39.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001548)

**[01:43:00.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001551)

**[01:43:06.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001552)

**[01:43:15.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001553)

**[01:44:22.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001561)

**[01:44:44.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001565)

**[01:44:45.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001566)

**[01:45:53.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001575)

**[01:45:58.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001576)

**[01:49:28.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001605)

**[01:51:29.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001629)

**[01:51:34.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001630)

**[01:51:46.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001632)  
Now, on to the third question, part of my question.

**[01:51:54.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001633)  
Malinaw po na nakasaad sa saligang batas natin,

**[01:51:57.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001634)  
and in fact, this has been enshrined in labor laws and other relevant labor laws,

**[01:52:03.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001635)

**[01:52:09.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001636)

**[01:52:14.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001637)  
Kung sa pampublikong sektor, collective negotiation ang tawag.

**[01:52:20.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001638)

**[01:52:24.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001639)

**[01:52:31.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001640)

**[01:52:35.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001641)

**[01:52:39.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001642)

**[01:52:43.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001643)

**[01:52:47.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001644)  
In CPCS 1 and CPCS 2,

**[01:52:51.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001645)

**[01:52:54.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001646)

**[01:53:03.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001647)

**[01:53:09.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001648)

**[01:53:13.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001649)

**[01:53:24.460] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001651)

**[01:53:28.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001652)

**[01:54:31.380] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001659)

**[01:54:49.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001660)

**[01:54:54.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001661)

**[01:55:24.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001662)

**[01:55:37.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001663)  
210, JR number 210.

**[01:55:39.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001664)  
210773, a rule that may not negotiate with their officers and employees the economic terms of their collective bargaining agreements.

**[01:55:54.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001665)  
And then for another Supreme Court ruling, PNCC versus NLRC, G.R. number 24...

**[01:56:03.030] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001665-split-2)

**[01:56:06.370] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001665-split-3)  
PNCC.

**[01:56:07.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001665-split-4)  
Okay, okay.

**[01:56:08.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001665-split-5)  
Yeah, PNCC versus NLRC, G.R. number 248401, said that with the subsequent enactment of RA 10149,

**[01:56:21.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001666)

**[01:56:24.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001667)  
Without persecuring the requisite authority of the President.

**[01:56:28.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001668)  
Without the authority of the President.

**[01:56:31.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001669)  
Yes.

**[01:56:32.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001670)  
Hayaan niyo po, Madam Sponsor, na reviewin din ang kinatawang ito, yung binanggit niyo po na jurisprudence on this matter.

**[01:56:39.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001671)

**[01:56:42.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001672)  
So as to reconcile our reading on this particular case.

**[01:56:48.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001673)  
Kasi napakalinaw na tila baga nadidefeat na.

**[01:56:54.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001674)

**[01:57:23.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001677)

**[01:57:24.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001678)

**[01:57:27.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001679)

will we get that commitment again from the distinguished sponsor and the GCG through its chairperson,

**[01:57:43.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001681)  
for us to sit down together and have an honest to goodness talk and dialogue about this matter and questions of workers in the GFIs?

**[01:57:54.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001682)  
Madam Sponsor?

**[01:57:56.740] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001683)  
Yes, we reaffirm the commitment and I was also informed by our good chairperson that this coming Friday, next Friday,

**[01:58:10.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001684)  
may meeting daw po with the labor groups firm, GFIs.

**[01:58:17.420] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001685)  
Thank you, Madam Sponsor and through the chairperson of GCG for affirming.

**[01:58:24.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001686)  
That commitment, dahil ako rin po ang kinatawang ito ay nais malinawan sa napakaraming mga tanong.

**[01:58:32.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001687)  
So thank you, Madam Sponsor, to my good friend and asahan ko po yung pagtutulungan natin on this matter.

**[01:58:40.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001688)  
And with that, Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor, there being no other member from the minority to interpolate for the budget of GCG,

**[01:58:53.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001689)  
I move.

**[01:58:54.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001690)  
To terminate the period of sponsorship and debate for the plenary consideration of the budget of the Governance Commission for GOCCs for Fiscal Year 2027.

**[01:59:06.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001691)  
I so move, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:59:08.600] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001692)  
What does the House say?

**[01:59:09.980] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001693)  
Majority Leader.

**[01:59:11.340] Rep. Rhea Mae A. Gullas** (asr-001694)  
Mr. Speaker, on the part of the majority, we join the minority to terminate the period of interpellation in the Governance Commission for GOCCs.

**[01:59:24.080] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001695)  
There is a joint motion to terminate the interpellations on the proposed budget of the Governance Commission for GOCCs.

**[01:59:34.240] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001696)  
Is there any objection?

**[01:59:36.520] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001697)  
The Chair hears none.

**[01:59:38.340] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001698)  
The interpellation on the proposed budget of the Governance Commission for GOCCs is hereby terminated.

**[01:59:46.940] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001699)  
Congratulations.

**[01:59:50.080] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001700)  
Majority Leader.

**[01:59:52.750] Rep. Rhea Mae A. Gullas** (asr-001701)  
Mr. Speaker, I move.

**[01:59:54.080] Rep. Rhea Mae A. Gullas** (asr-001702)  
To consider the proposed budget of the Mindanao Development Authority.

**[02:00:00.000] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001703)  
Is there any objection? The Chair hears none. Motion is approved. Majority Leader.

**[02:00:06.400] Rep. Rhea Mae A. Gullas** (asr-001704)  
Mr. Speaker, may we acknowledge the heads and officials of the Mindanao Development Authority, Chairperson Leo Magno, Usec Janet Lopoz, Asec Romeo Montenegro. I so move, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:00:24.960] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001705)  
To the family of the Mindanao Development Authority, headed by the Chairperson, Secretary Leo Tereso Magno, welcome to the House of Representatives. Majority Leader.

**[02:00:40.840] Rep. Rhea Mae A. Gullas** (asr-001706)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that you recognize the Honorable Nicanor Briones, Vice Chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations to sponsor the proposed budget of the Mindanao Development Authority.

**[02:00:54.170] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001707)  
The Honorable Representative Niki Briones is recognized. Please proceed.

**[02:01:01.850] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001708)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:01:04.220] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001709)  
Dear colleagues, ladies and gentlemen, good evening.

**[02:01:08.680] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001710)  
I rise today to sponsor the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget of the Mindanao Development Authority, headed by Secretary Leo Tereso Magno.

**[02:01:21.500] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001711)  
Mindanao Development Authority is mandated.

**[02:01:24.120] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001712)  
By virtue of Republic Act No. 9996, to promote, coordinate, and facilitate the socio-economic development of Mindanao through an integrated approach promoting trade, tourism, investment, private enterprises, and peace.

**[02:01:45.840] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001713)  
It also serves as the permanent Philippine Coordinating Office for the Brunei Darussalam-Indonesia-Malaysia-Philippines East ASEAN Growth Area.

**[02:01:54.120] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001714)  
It acts as the Secretariat for the Mindanao Power Monitoring Committee, Mindanao River Basin Management Council, NTF-ELCAC, and Cabinet Officer for Regional Development and Security in Mindanao.

**[02:02:15.840] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001715)  
It also implements critical strategic programs for the Mindanao.

**[02:02:24.120] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001716)  
Such as the Project RIBER, which helps local government units develop, rehabilitate, and maintain its water supply infrastructure.

**[02:02:35.060] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001717)  
These programs are aligned with the Mindanao Agenda, which aims to achieve inclusive economic recovery, sustain green growth, build resilience, and strengthen peace and development for an improved quality of life for every Mindanao citizen.

**[02:02:54.120] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001718)  
[unclear] by 2030. For this, we respectfully seek this Chamber's support to consider and approve the proposed budget of the Mindanao Development Authority for fiscal year 2027, amounting to ₱272,435,000. Together, let us help a better Mindanao. Thank you very much, and I now welcome any questions from our colleagues.

**[02:03:26.840] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001719)  
Thank you, Honorable Briones. Majority Leader.

**[02:03:30.620] Rep. Rhea Mae A. Gullas** (asr-001719-split-2)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Presley De Jesus for his interpellation.

**[02:03:35.970] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-001720)  
The Honorable Presley De Jesus of Party-list PHILRECA is recognized. Please proceed.

**[02:03:43.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001721)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker. Of course, to the distinguished sponsor, this is about the Mindanao Energy Security.

**[02:03:51.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001722)  
Question. Mr. Speaker, this is the sponsor. Based on the projects, Minda has identified reliable and affordable electricity necessary for their success and sustainability.

**[02:04:36.720] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001723)  
What's your question?

**[02:04:38.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001724)  
Okay, let me repeat my question. Based on the projects, MinDA has identified reliable and affordable electricity necessary for their success and sustainability. In short, naka-rely po kayo sa success ng the Mindanao project, yung affordable electricity for their success and sustainability. Yes or no?

**[02:05:04.540] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001725)  
Yes, distinguished colleague.

**[02:05:07.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001726)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker. Does Minda recognize electricity as a foundational requirement for Mindanao's industrial, agricultural, commercial, educational, health, and social development?

**[02:05:21.540] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001727)  
Yes, distinguished colleague.

**[02:05:25.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001728)  
Can MinDA describe what they know about the Agus-Pulangi hydropower complex?

**[02:05:34.400] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001729)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:05:40.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001730)  
Sir, can you describe what they know?

**[02:05:43.700] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001731)  
We support all our efforts for the rehabilitation of the Agus Pulangi.

**[02:05:51.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001732)  
So, are you familiar with the Agus-Pulangi hydropower complex?

**[02:05:59.940] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001733)  
Yes, distinguished colleague.

**[02:06:03.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001734)  
Alam niyo po, ang Agus-Pulangi, yan po yung matagal na pong pinagkukunan, source of energy sa Mindanao.

**[02:06:10.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001735)  
That's why Congressman Sergio Dagooc, passionate siya pagdating po rito.

**[02:06:15.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001736)  
So, ang tanong ko po, because as we know, marami.

**[02:06:21.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001737)  
Marami po nag-aahangad sa Agus-Pulangi na ma-privatize.

**[02:06:26.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001738)  
So, do the Mindanao officers ng Mindanao Development Authority, are you in favor of, on record, are you in favor of privatizing Agus-Pulangi?

**[02:06:46.440] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001739)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[02:06:51.540] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001740)  
What the Mindanao Development Authority wants is rehabilitation, not privatization.

**[02:06:59.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001741)  
So, in short, you're in favor or you're not in favor of privatizing Agus-Pulangi?

**[02:07:05.220] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001742)  
No, no. Mindanao is not in favor.

**[02:07:07.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001743)  
So, you're not in favor?

**[02:07:09.680] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001744)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:07:10.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001745)  
Second, so, as we know, that Agus-Pulangi hydropower needs rehabilitation.

**[02:07:19.780] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001746)  
Yeah, yes.

**[02:07:21.540] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001747)  
As of now, the capacity of the Agus-Pulangi hydropower plant is 1,000 megawatts and it's only running 600 to 700 megawatts, distinguished colleague.

**[02:07:42.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001748)  
So, in short, again, let me do some fun facts.

**[02:07:47.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001749)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor.

**[02:07:51.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001750)  
The National Power Corporation identifies the Agus-Pulangi complexes as having a combined installed capacity of 982 megawatts

**[02:08:04.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001751)  
and supplying more than 50% of Mindanao's electricity requirements.

**[02:08:10.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001752)  
Alam po natin kung ano po ang kalagayan ng Agus-Pulangi.

**[02:08:14.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001753)  
Dito po nanggagaling kalahati po ng supply sa Mindanao.

**[02:08:19.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001754)  
Kaya, pansinin niyo po.

**[02:08:21.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001755)  
Bisayas, nagkakaroon ng yellow alert.

**[02:08:24.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001756)  
Even na Luzon, pero ang Bisayas, ang Mindanao ay bihira po because of Agus-Pulangi.

**[02:08:33.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001757)  
Does Mindanao Development Authority agree that preserving affordable and reliable access to this renewable energy resource is essential to Mindanao development?

**[02:08:48.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001758)  
Do you recognize po yan?

**[02:08:49.780] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001759)  
Definitely, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:08:51.540] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001760)  
Distinguished colleague.

**[02:08:55.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001761)  
Thank you.

**[02:08:56.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001762)  
If electricity is vital to Mindanao's development and Agus-Pulangi is a major government-owned source of electricity,

**[02:09:08.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001763)  
what precisely is Mindanao's position on private participation in its rehabilitation operation and management arrangement?

**[02:09:16.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001764)  
Ano ba yung arrangement nyo?

**[02:09:18.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001765)  
Kasi, ano po, rehabilitate na.

**[02:09:21.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001766)  
Ano po ang operation and management?

**[02:09:24.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001767)  
Ano po ang arrangement po natin dito?

**[02:09:49.640] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001768)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[02:09:51.540] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001769)  
Distinguished colleague, iba ang ahensya na mag-iimplement ng privatization. Yung PSALM ang siyang mag-iimplement ng privatization [unclear].

**[02:10:17.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001770)  
So, Mr. Speaker, medyo gusto ko lang linawin ito. Are we going to privatize ang Agus-Pulangi o yung kanilang rehabilitation lang?

**[02:10:27.900] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001771)  
It's only for rehabilitation.

**[02:10:30.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001772)  
Okay. So, malinaw po, Mr. Speaker, on record, the Agus-Pulangi will not be privatized but only yung rehabilitation ang ibibigay sa private. Tama ba ako doon?

**[02:10:46.100] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001773)  
Tama po.

**[02:10:47.340] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001774)  
Mr. Speaker, yun ang gagawin.

**[02:10:51.820] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001775)  
Yun ang gagawin, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:10:54.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001776)  
Okay. This is very important.

**[02:10:58.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001777)  
Section 47F of EPIRA excluded Agus-Pulangi from the generation assets initially privatized, provided that the ownership will be transferred to PSALM and the hydropower complexes will continue to be operated

**[02:11:17.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001780)  
by NPC. It further states that privatization is left to PSALM's discretion in consultation with Congress.

**[02:11:30.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001781)  
Do you agree with that?

**[02:11:30.920] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001782)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:11:33.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001783)  
We have to kasi. Nasa EPIRA law too. Section 47F, no?

**[02:11:38.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001784)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor, meron po bang naganap na konsultasyon sa Kongreso?

**[02:11:46.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001785)  
So, patuloy.

**[02:11:47.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001786)  
Kung tungkol sa rehabilitation ng Agus-Pulangi, as far as I know, wala po akong alam na [unclear] hindi po ito kasama sa mandato ng Mindanao [unclear].

**[02:12:04.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001787)  
Ang gusto ko lang po dito malaman ay kung ano po talaga ang position ng Minda sa pangangalagaan natin sa nag-iisa na lamang na government-owned.

**[02:12:17.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001788)  
Ito na lang po ang natitira. Kasi lahat po ng generation naibenta na ng PSALM. Ito na lang po ang kaisa-isa, ang Agus-Pulangi sa Mindanao.

**[02:12:28.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001789)  
As the Development Authority in Mindanao, would you allow the vital and strategic energy asset na mapunta na lamang sa kamay ng private corporation?

**[02:12:39.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001790)  
Again, you said you are not willing to turn it over or to sell it to private corporation.

**[02:12:47.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001791)  
Am I right?

**[02:12:49.380] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001792)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, tama po yun.

**[02:12:57.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001793)  
Yes, tama. Hindi natin ibebenta.

**[02:12:59.560] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001794)  
Hindi pwedeng ibebenta.

**[02:13:00.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001795)  
That would be my five times na I asked you and you said yes, na hindi ibebenta. Thank you for that.

**[02:13:07.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001796)  
Follow-up question, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:13:10.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001797)  
May ginawa po ba ang MinDA para makasiguro na ang mga tao sa Agus, sa Lanao, sa Bukidnon

**[02:13:17.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001798)  
sa Iligan o sa iba pang mga apektadong komunidad na kasama sila sa decision-making patungkol dito sa Agus-Pulangi?

**[02:13:26.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001799)  
Sigurado po ako ay importante po itong issue na ito para sa ating mga local communities.

**[02:13:32.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001800)  
Have you ever engaged the people from Lanao, Bukidno, even in Iligan?

**[02:13:39.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001801)  
Pagdating po rito, nagkaroon ba ng public consultation? Even yung rehabilitation lang?

**[02:13:47.340] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001802)  
Considering that Minda is a member of the Power Monitoring Committee, nagkaroon po ng consultation.

**[02:13:55.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001803)  
So there was consultation happen?

**[02:13:59.960] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001804)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:14:01.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001805)  
When? Where?

**[02:14:11.980] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001807)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, every year, nagkakaroon ng...

**[02:14:17.340] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001808)  
Mindanao Power Summit, kasama ang lahat ng stakeholders sa summit.

**[02:14:26.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001809)  
So every year, there's a consultation. Was the consultation included or it being mentioned, the privatization or even the rehabilitation of Agus-Pulangi? Kasama po ba yan

**[02:14:42.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001810)  
Pagkaano kayo?

**[02:14:44.160] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001811)  
Kasama, kasama yung...

**[02:14:45.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001812)  
Habang nagkakaroon kayo?

**[02:14:47.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001813)  
In February na summit.

**[02:14:49.220] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001814)  
Kasama yung rehabilitation?

**[02:14:52.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001815)  
And the privatization.

**[02:15:00.000] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001816)  
At tuloy-tuloy ang pag-uusap tungkol sa Agus-Pulangi, kasama ang Department of Energy.

**[02:15:08.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001817)  
When you say continuous dialogue, ano ho yung pinag-uusapan?

**[02:15:17.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001818)  
In privatization ho ba o hindi?

**[02:15:28.130] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001819)  
Only privatization of maintenance and rehabilitation.

**[02:15:33.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001820)  
So ano ho ang sabi ng mga tao from Bukidnon, Iligan, and Lanao? Are they in favor of privatization of Agus-Pulangi?

**[02:16:02.300] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001821)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, ang gusto ng taong bayan ay rehabilitation.

**[02:16:10.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001821-split-2)  
Rehabilitation?

**[02:16:12.120] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001821-split-3)  
At continuously makapag-serve ang Agus-Pulangi.

**[02:16:17.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001821-split-4)  
The Mindanao people wanted?

**[02:16:18.870] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001821-split-5)  
One hundred percent.

**[02:16:20.510] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001822)  
Rehabilitation, but not privatization of Agus-Pulangi.

**[02:16:23.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001823)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, for that. At least natutuwa ako, lalo na si Kong Serge Dagooc na nanonood ngayon sa atin.

**[02:16:32.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001824)  
Dahil nandun siya sa Mindanao ngayon. Umiiyak siguro habang pinanonood tayo na sa Agus-Pulangi yung tao.

**[02:16:39.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001825)  
He's a very dedicated person, being a lineman himself.

**[02:16:43.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001826)  
My next question.

**[02:16:49.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001827)  
Given Mindanao Development Authority mandate to promote, coordinate, and facilitate Mindanao's socioeconomic development,

**[02:16:58.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001828)  
is the agency truly protecting the interests of Mindanao when it supports or tolerates an arrangement that may place a major government-owned source of electricity under private control?

**[02:17:12.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001829)  
I guess, nasagot mo naman na ito, so we don't have to answer that. At least a sign of relief.

**[02:17:19.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001830)  
Did MinDA ever submit a written recommendation to the President, DOE, PSALM, NPC, even Congress, or any other responsible body concerning Agus-Pulangi?

**[02:17:34.540] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001831)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:17:42.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001832)  
Does that recommendation prioritize the long-term welfare of Mindanao residents?

**[02:17:49.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001833)  
Over short-term fiscal proceeds or private investor returns?

**[02:17:55.320] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-001834)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:17:58.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001835)  
Again, Mr. Speaker, before Congress approves its proposed fiscal year 2027 budget, Mindanao Development Authority should assure this August body that it is genuinely prioritizing the welfare of the people of Mindanao.

**[02:18:19.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001836)  
And is committed to the inclusive and sustainable development of the region.

**[02:18:24.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001837)  
That commitment must be demonstrated not only through plans, meetings, and policy statements, but through the protection of Mindanao's strategic public assets, particularly Agus-Pulangi.

**[02:18:43.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001838)  
These facilities are essential to Mindanao.

**[02:18:49.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001839)  
Mindanao's energy security and development.

**[02:18:52.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001840)  
They must remain subject to public accountability and must not be placed under the control of private entities at the expense of consumers, electric, cooperatives, host communities, and the broader public interest.

**[02:19:10.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001841)  
Accordingly, the Mindanao Development Authority should clearly and unequivocally state that it will not support

**[02:19:19.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001842)  
any arrangement that transfers effective control of Agus-Pulangi to private corporations without transparent justification, congressional consultation, strong consumer safeguards, and continuing public control.

**[02:19:38.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001843)  
Mindanao should also assure this committee that it is not siding with private corporations for their own commercial or financial interests.

**[02:19:49.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001850)  
The recommendation must be based solely on its statutory mandate and the long-term welfare of the people of Mindanao.

**[02:19:57.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001851)  
Minda should have a clear commitment to remain independent, transparent, accountable, and firmly aligned with the people of Mindanao.

**[02:20:07.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001852)  
Before I end my interpellation, Mr. Speaker, Congressman Dagooc and I strongly oppose the privatization of Agus-Pulangi.

**[02:20:21.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001853)  
Yan na lang po ang nag-iisang generation po ng ating gobyerno.

**[02:20:29.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001854)  
Sana manatili pong yan government-owned para po sa ating mga kababayan sa Mindanao.

**[02:20:35.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001855)  
Again, thank you very much, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:20:37.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001856)  
And of course, to the good-looking sponsor and to the rest of Mindanao Development Authority family.

**[02:20:47.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001857)

**[02:37:13.460] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002132)  
Finally, distinguished sponsor, anong mekanism ang pinapairal ng MCB

**[02:37:18.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002139)  
para ang compensation process ay hindi lamang mabilis kundi transparent at free from bureaucratic delays or inconsistencies?

**[02:37:39.000] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002140)  
Mr. Speaker, as a quasi-judicial body, we have to ensure na tama at eligible ang mga babayaran.

**[02:37:48.800] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002141)  
But from time to time, we regularly review and streamline the process. The process generally involves filing, technical and legal evaluation, clarification hearing when necessary, deliberation and decision by the Board, posting, publication, and awarding and payment. For structural claims there must first be technical assessment,

**[02:38:18.780] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002142)  
which may involve validation and ocular inspection. Claims then undergo legal evaluation before being raffled to a division for adjudication. We have streamlined the process by allowing claims below 10 million to be decided at the division level instead of requiring every claim to undergo en banc deliberation, thereby removing a significant [unclear].

**[02:38:48.760] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002143)  
Aside from regular reporting and monitoring of accomplishment, mentioned earlier including assessment of quota rates, available din po ang mga reporting data through the website.

**[02:39:02.400] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002144)  
That's to be transparent.

**[02:39:06.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002145)  
Yes.

**[02:39:06.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002146)

**[02:39:32.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002149)

na claimants ng competition.

**[02:51:54.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002229)

**[02:51:58.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002230)

**[02:52:10.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002231)

**[02:52:16.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002232)

**[02:52:21.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002233)

**[02:52:40.320] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002234)

**[02:52:49.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002235)
