**[02:52:50.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002236)

**[02:52:54.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002237)

**[02:53:01.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002238)

**[02:53:09.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002239)

**[02:53:18.580] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002240)  
Willing po ang MCV na...

**[02:53:20.860] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002241)

**[02:53:33.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002242)

**[02:53:43.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002243)  
para po yung ating mga naging issue na ina-identify ay mabilis po.

**[02:53:50.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002244)

**[02:53:58.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002245)

Majority Leader.

**[02:54:02.280] Rep. Daphne A. Lagon** (asr-002247)  
Madam Speaker, may recognize Representative Sara Elago for her interpellation.

**[02:54:07.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002248)

**[02:54:13.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002249)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker.

**[02:54:15.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002250)

**[02:54:20.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002251)  
PONDO ng MCB.

**[02:54:24.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002252)  
Ayon ho sa ulat ng MCB, as of August 31, 2026, mayroon itong 4.362 billion total appropriations,

**[02:54:34.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002253)

**[02:54:38.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002254)  
Nakasaad din na mayroon pang 2 billion pesos na unallocated amount pending further release from DBM.

**[02:54:45.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002255)

**[02:54:47.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002256)

**[02:54:50.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002257)  
Parang 2 billion pesos.

**[02:55:03.780] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002258)

**[02:55:14.400] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002259)

**[02:55:19.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002260)  
At Madam Speaker,

**[02:55:20.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002261)  
ay ma-re-release na ito.

**[02:55:22.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002262)

**[02:55:30.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002263)  
Lalong-lalo na para sa 2027 ay napakaliit lamang ng kanilang pondo na naisama sa General Appropriations Bill

**[02:55:38.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002264)  
habang napakaraming mga pending pang-claims.

**[02:55:43.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002265)  
Ngayon, ang MCB po ay nailikha sa ilalim ng Republic Act No. 11696 at nagsimula.

**[02:55:50.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002266)  
Ang operations noong January 30, 2023.

**[02:55:56.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002267)  
Nakatakdang matapos ang five-year mandate nito sa June 3, 2028.

**[02:56:00.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002268)  
As of August 2026, may halos dalawang taon na lamang ang board.

**[02:56:06.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002269)  
Sa kasalukuyang mga backlog at funding requirements na narinig po natin sa ating deliberasyon ngayong gabi,

**[02:56:13.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002270)

**[02:56:23.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002271)

**[02:56:28.020] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002272)  
Your Honor, Madam Speaker,

**[02:56:30.960] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002273)

**[02:56:38.900] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002274)  
ang hindi lang nila magagawa ay yung makapag-disburse dahil kulang ang ating pondong inilaan

**[02:56:48.860] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002275)  
para sa mga...

**[02:56:50.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002276)  
Narinig natin, Madam Speaker, na kulang ang pondo.

**[02:56:55.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002277)  
At kung hindi matatapos ang lahat ng claims bago ang June 2028,

**[02:57:01.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002278)

**[02:57:05.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002279)  
Sino ang magpapatuloy ng proseso ng pagtitiyak ng compensation,

**[02:57:12.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002280)

**[02:57:18.000] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002281)

**[02:57:20.860] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002282)

**[02:57:26.680] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002283)

**[02:57:37.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002284)

**[02:57:39.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002285)  
Ngunit, paano po maapektuhan ang inyong trabaho ng Executive Order No. 78

**[02:57:47.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002286)

**[02:57:50.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002287)  
Sabi po dito ang magsisilbi na sekretariat

**[02:57:56.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002288)

**[02:58:07.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002289)  
para daw po sa policy and program coordination

**[02:58:10.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002290)  
para matiyak ang consistency ng goals at objectives ng MCB

**[02:58:16.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002291)  
sa lahat ng policies at trust ng national government.

**[02:58:19.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002292)  
Ibig sabihin po ba nito?

**[02:58:20.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002293)  
Maski po yung role ninyo bilang attached sa Office of the Presidential Advisor for Marawi Rehabilitation and Development

**[02:58:29.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002294)  
ay hanggang 2028 na lamang po?

**[02:58:38.860] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002295)  
Mukhang ganun po ang mangyayari dahil yung MCB wala nang tapos na yung kanilang lifespan.

**[02:58:50.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002296)  
Madam Speaker, kung ang mga miyembro ngayon ng Marawi Compensation Board

**[02:58:56.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002297)  
ang pipili at poposisyon, sa tingin niyo po ba dapat i-extend ang mandato ng Marawi Compensation Board

**[02:59:03.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002298)  
hanggat sa hindi natatapos yung pagbabayad ng mga remaining claims?

**[02:59:09.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002299)  
Siyempre po, dapat matiyak na matapos ito sa takdang panahon at hindi yung pangmatagalan.

**[02:59:16.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002300)  
Kasi yun na nga yung nangyari po ngayon.

**[02:59:18.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002301)  
Natumagal na.

**[02:59:19.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002302)  
Na halos magsasampung taon na, nang hindi pa rin natitiyak yung mabilis na pagbabayad ng mga claims.

**[02:59:26.860] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002303)  
Mas maganda sana kung makakaroon ng extension pero siguro makangailangan niya ng batas.

**[02:59:35.860] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002304)  
May mga bills ang alam ko sa Congress at sa Senate to extend the life of Marawi Compensation Board.

**[02:59:47.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002305)  
Madam Speaker, ang datos mismo ng Marawi Compensation Board ay nagpapakita na malaki na ang pondong nailabas para sa Compensation Program.

**[02:59:57.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002306)  
Ngunit mayroon pa rin...

**[03:00:00.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002307)  
mas malaking bilang pa ng claims na kailangang risolbahin at pondohan.

**[03:00:05.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002308)  
As of August 2026, 5,945 claims remain pending.

**[03:00:12.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002309)  
Sa ating 2027 General Appropriations Bill, naglalaan lamang ng 1 billion pesos para sa compensation program

**[03:00:19.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002310)  
kumpara sa malaking kinakailangan na 5.28 billion proposal ng board.

**[03:00:27.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002311)  
Ang funding gap ay tumataginting po napakalaki na 4.015 billion pesos.

**[03:00:35.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002312)  
Kaya napakahalagang malinaw na hindi lamang kung gaano karaming claims ang naprocess,

**[03:00:41.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002313)  
kundi dapat may sapat at tiyak na pondo para sa lahat ng claimants,

**[03:00:46.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002314)  
na matitiyak natin na may mekanismo ng transparency,

**[03:00:51.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002315)  
matitiyak na ang bawat piso ng pondo ay madidiretsyo mismo sa mga claimants

**[03:00:56.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002316)  
na ating mga kababayan na apektado bago matapos ang mandato ng board.

**[03:01:02.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002317)  
At bilang panghuli, kinatawagan na rin natin ang Kongreso na ito na magpatawag po sana tayo

**[03:01:08.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002318)  
ng pagdinig tungkol sa potensyal sa posible na pag-extend ng taon ng mandato ng Marawi Compensation Board

**[03:01:19.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002319)  
para lang po sa interes ng karapatang pantao, para sa interes ng justisya,

**[03:01:25.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002320)  
para sa interes.

**[03:01:26.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002321)  
ng pagtitiyak na nga nararapat na kompensasyon at reparasyon sa lahat ng mga 2017 Marawi Siege internally displaced persons.

**[03:01:37.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002322)  
Maraming maraming salamat Madam Speaker. Maraming salamat sa ating distinguished sponsor.

**[03:01:42.380] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002323)  
Majority Leader.

**[03:01:45.100] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-002324)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Antonio Tinio.

**[03:01:52.840] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002324-split-2)  
The Honorable Tinio is recognized

**[03:01:56.030] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002325)  
of ACT Teachers for his interpellation

**[03:01:57.400] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-002326)  
on the proposed budget of the MCB.

**[03:02:02.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002327)  
Thank you Madam Speaker. Madam Speaker, maraming na pong natanong at pasisimple na lang po natin

**[03:02:12.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002328)  
at ididin yung mga importanteng punto kaugnay sa budget ng Marawi Compensation Board.

**[03:02:21.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002329)  
So, pakikita nga po natin na ang proposed budget ay

**[03:02:26.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002330)  
1.247 billion pesos at sa halagang yun ay 1 billion pesos ang para sa Marawi Siege Victims Compensation Fund.

**[03:02:41.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002331)  
So, nabanggit na po na papalapit na ang katapusan ng mandato ng Marawi Compensation Board.

**[03:02:52.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002332)  
So, sabi nga doon sa Republic Act 11696.

**[03:02:56.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002333)  
Merong sunset clause.

**[03:02:59.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002334)  
Diba?

**[03:03:00.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002335)  
At sabi rito,

**[03:03:01.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002336)  
The board shall complete its work within five years from the effectivity of the IRR.

**[03:03:08.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002337)  
After such period, it shall become punctus officio.

**[03:03:13.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002338)  
Pagkatapos nun, ipapasa na sa DSUD.

**[03:03:20.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002339)  
The DSUD shall continue to exercise the powers and functions of the board.

**[03:03:26.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002341)  
to disburse and process any and all pending compensation due to qualified claimants.

**[03:03:33.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002342)  
So, ganun po ang nakatakda sa batas.

**[03:03:35.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002343)  
Of course, dapat ituring ang sunset clause na ito bilang hamon sa kasalukuyang board.

**[03:03:45.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002344)  
Huwag na nating intayin na umabot yung deadline at may maiwan pang trabaho sa DSUD.

**[03:03:53.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002345)  
So, ngayon,

**[03:03:56.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002346)  
ulitin lang natin,

**[03:03:59.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002347)  
ang Pondo for Compensation ngayon ay 1 billion.

**[03:04:03.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002348)  
So, malinaw po, nadiinan na, kapos ito.

**[03:04:07.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002349)  
Magkano po ang kailangan pa para makompleto ang trabaho ng compensation

**[03:04:14.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002350)  
bago marating yung dulo o wakas ng mandato

**[03:04:23.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002351)  
ng

**[03:04:26.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002354)  
Marawi Compensation Board? Ito po ay sa

**[03:04:29.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002355)  
after May 31, 2028. Tama po ba?

**[03:04:33.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002356)  
Tama po.

**[03:04:36.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002357)  
At magkano po ang kabuuang kailangan para sa ilang bilang ng mga victims?

**[03:04:43.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002358)  
Climants?

**[03:04:44.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002359)  
Mangangailangan po tayo, Madam Speaker,

**[03:04:47.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002360)  
Mr. Sponsor,

**[03:04:49.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002361)  
distinguished colleague,

**[03:04:51.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002362)  
na mga 6.6 billion

**[03:04:54.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002363)  
on top of 1 billion.

**[03:04:55.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002364)  
So, total kailangan, 7.6 billion for 2027, 1 billion lamang

**[03:05:05.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002365)  
ang nakalaan. At muli, yung 1 billion ay para sa ilang bilang po ito muli ng claimants?

**[03:05:15.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002366)  
4,360 claimants.

**[03:05:19.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002367)  
4,000?

**[03:05:21.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002368)  
For the total, 7.6 billion.

**[03:05:24.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002369)  
Okay.

**[03:05:25.680] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002370)  
Ang claimants na pwedeng mabigyan is 4,300.

**[03:05:30.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002371)  
4,300. So, yung 1 billion po, ilan lang po ang mabibigyan diyan?

**[03:05:34.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002372)  
760.

**[03:05:38.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002373)  
760 lamang po. So, well, sa proposed budget for 2027, nakatali po ang kamay

**[03:05:52.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002374)  
ng Marawi Compensation Board.

**[03:05:56.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002375)  
700, ilan po?

**[03:05:59.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002376)  
760 claimants.

**[03:06:02.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002377)  
760 claimants lang po ang mabibigyan.

**[03:06:06.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002378)  
So, ang usapin lang naman po rito ay ano ba ang kakayanin ng Marawi Compensation Board?

**[03:06:20.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002379)  
Padaling sabihin, dagdagan natin ang pondo pero kaya bang ma-award ng Marawi Compensation Board ang mas malaking bilang kesa dito sa 760 families?

**[03:06:41.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002380)  
Ano po ang kayang gawin?

**[03:06:44.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002381)  
Distinguished colleague, Madam Speaker, kayang-kaya po yan. Ang kailangan lang po natin ay pondo.

**[03:06:54.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002382)  
Kayang-kaya. Magbigay po tayo ng kongkretong numero. Ano ang kakayanin?

**[03:07:02.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002383)  
Actually, yung 760, one to two months.

**[03:07:08.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002384)  
In one to two months ay kaya po yan?

**[03:07:11.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002385)  
Kaya bigyan.

**[03:07:12.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002386)  
Opo.

**[03:07:13.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002387)  
Kung may pondo po tayo, eh bago mag-lapse yung mandato, mapoprovide po natin, mabibigyan po natin yung lahat ng claimants.

**[03:07:29.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002388)  
Baka may maibigay po kayong mas kongkretong target na kakayanin realistically sa isang taon, fiscal year 2027.

**[03:07:43.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002389)  
We request the distinguished sponsor to give an estimate. Ilan ang kakayanin?

**[03:07:52.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002390)  
3,500 sa isang taon, Your Honor.

**[03:07:57.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002391)  
3,500. Hindi lang 760, kundi 3,500 claimants ang kayang mabigyan.

**[03:08:06.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002392)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:08:07.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002393)  
Okay. Magkano po ang kailangan pondo para sa 3,500 na yan?

**[03:08:12.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002394)  
5 billion, Madam Speaker.

**[03:08:20.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002395)  
5 billion pesos. Okay. So, kung may 1 billion na kailangan dagdagan ng 4, ganun po ba?

**[03:08:27.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002396)  
Tama po. Tama po.

**[03:08:28.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002397)  
4 billion pesos additional.

**[03:08:30.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002398)  
4 billion.

**[03:08:31.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002399)  
Okay. Well, Madam Speaker, malinaw naman po narinig natin na sabi ng Marawi Compensation Board,

**[03:08:40.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002400)  
kaya daw nila makapagbigay, mag-award to 3,600 families at mangangailangan ng additional 4 billion pesos.

**[03:08:55.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002401)  
So, ito po ba ay naparating ng Marawi Compensation Board sa Committee on Appropriations sa takdang panahon?

**[03:09:10.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002402)  
May we get a yes or no from the sponsor?

**[03:09:14.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002403)  
Mukhang nagsend ng letter.

**[03:09:21.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002404)  
Nagsend ng letter. Okay. So, malinaw po, Madam Speaker.

**[03:09:25.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002405)  
So, ang kinatawang ito ay sumusuporta po sa request for institutional amendment ng Marawi Compensation Board

**[03:09:37.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002406)  
for an additional 4 billion, pero pangahawakan po natin ang kanilang deklarasyon dito na kaya nilang ma-award ang pondo,

**[03:09:53.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002407)  
ang dagdag na pondo to 3,600.

**[03:09:58.850] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002407-split-2)  
Tama, tama.

**[03:09:59.410] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002407-split-3)  
3,600 claimants.

**[03:10:05.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002408)  
So, yun po ang pinakamahalaga.

**[03:10:07.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002409)  
Ngayon, kanina meron pong mga youth from Marawi.

**[03:10:14.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002410)  
Nagpunta pa sila dito sa Maynila para nga makaharap ang Marawi Compensation Board.

**[03:10:23.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002411)  
At hinaharap naman tayo ni Chair Macarambon at ng kanyang mga staff.

**[03:10:30.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002412)  
Papasalamat po tayo doon.

**[03:10:32.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002413)  
Isa sa mga urgent request po nila ay yung director.

**[03:10:36.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002414)  
Ay yung direktang harapan, dialogue.

**[03:10:39.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002415)  
Dahil kailangan-kailangan ng opisyal at klarong informasyon tungkol sa ano na ba ang kalagayan

**[03:10:50.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002416)  
ng mga matagal nang iniintay na pag-award ng claims.

**[03:10:57.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002417)  
At gusto lang po natin mailagay on record na may commitment po

**[03:11:05.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002418)  
for a dialogue in Marawi led by the Marawi Compensation Board.

**[03:11:11.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002419)  
Ang tinarget po doon ay first week of October.

**[03:11:16.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002420)  
So, confirm po ba yung commitment na iyon?

**[03:11:20.200] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-002421)  
Yes, Your Honor. Confirm po yung commitment.

**[03:11:23.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002422)  
Thank you. Maraming salamat po. So, yun lang po.

**[03:11:27.770] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002423)  
Thank you very much, Honorable Tinio, Majority Leader.

**[03:11:29.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002424)  
Yun lang po, Madam Speaker, nagpapasalamat tayo.

**[03:11:32.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002425)  
Muli, sinusuportahan natin.

**[03:11:34.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002426)  
Yung request ng Marawi Compensation Board for additional 4 billion pesos

**[03:11:43.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002427)  
para magampanan nila ang kanilang mandato.

**[03:11:46.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002428)  
And we look forward to their commitment na haharapin yung mga mamamayan ng Marawi sa Marawi mismo

**[03:11:55.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002429)  
pagbalik nila doon sa Oktubre.

**[03:11:57.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002430)  
Hanggang doon na lamang po, Madam Speaker. Maraming salamat.

**[03:12:00.660] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002431)  
May be the Honorable Tinio would also like to move for the termination of the meeting.

**[03:12:05.350] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002432)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[03:12:07.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002433)  
There being no other member of the minority who wishes to interpolate,

**[03:12:12.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002434)  
I move for the termination of the period of interpolation and debate

**[03:12:19.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002435)  
on the budget of the Marawi Compensation Board.

**[03:12:23.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002436)  
Majority Leader.

**[03:12:24.200] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-002437)  
Madam Speaker, on the part of the majority, we join the minority in the motion to terminate

**[03:12:30.200] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-002438)  
the period of interpolation of the proposal, Marawi Compensation Board.

**[03:12:33.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002439)  
There is a joint motion to terminate the period of interpellations

**[03:12:39.680] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002440)  
on the proposed budget of the Marawi Compensation Board.

**[03:12:43.660] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002441)

**[03:12:45.160] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002442)

Fiscal Year 2027 Budget

**[03:14:34.600] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002477)  
of the Bases Conversion and Development Authority.

**[03:14:40.460] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002478)

**[03:14:45.760] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002479)  
and the BCDA family,

**[03:14:48.760] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002480)  
we are ready to respond to the questions

**[03:14:51.140] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002481)  
of our esteemed colleagues during the interpellation.

**[03:14:56.160] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002482)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[03:15:00.000] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-002483)  
Madam Speaker, may we recognize the Honorable Dada Kiram Ismula of the Akbayan Partylist for her interpellation.

**[03:15:10.320] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002484)  
The Honorable Dada Kiram Ismula is hereby recognized.

**[03:15:16.460] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002485)  
Assalamualaikum warahmatullahi wabarakatuhu.

**[03:15:20.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002486)  
Dediretso na po ako sa aking katanungan para po mabilis tayong matapos dahil 10.13 na rin po ng gabi.

**[03:15:30.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002487)  
Nakasaad sa gab na iisa lamang ang line item ng BCDA para sa susunod na taon.

**[03:15:37.740] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002488)  
Malinaw po sa proposed appropriation na ang buong 1.983 billion ay para sa implementation ng BCDA Infrastructure Development Program at kaugnay nito,

**[03:15:53.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002489)  
ano ang specific na performance target ng BCDA na dapat ma-achieve?

**[03:16:00.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002490)  
Sa ilalim ng program na ito.

**[03:16:03.640] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002491)  
Thank you Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[03:16:06.700] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002492)  
Ang targets po for 2027, ang isa pong target is the number of generated employment, 1,500.

**[03:16:15.960] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002493)  
The percentage of completed facilities constructed for the AFP, 100%, such as the Philippine Marine Headquarters, the Philippine Navy Senior Officers Quarters,

**[03:16:28.880] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002497)  
And the percentage of infrastructure projects implemented as planned, 100% din po.

**[03:16:35.120] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002498)  
Such as the connecting road to the virology of DOST, the National Academy of Sports Dormitory of DepEd,

**[03:16:42.360] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002499)  
the access road leading to the Central Bank, University of the Philippines, and Philippine High School.

**[03:16:50.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002500)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[03:16:53.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002501)  
As we all know, ang issue ng Paxilica ay nangangailangan ng kalinawan.

**[03:17:00.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002502)  
Iilang countries ang papasok dito sa Clark Advanced Manufacturing Park under sa Paxilica.

**[03:17:07.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002503)  
Ilan po?

**[03:17:09.020] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002504)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[03:17:10.680] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002505)  
Sa ngayon po, nothing is final yet.

**[03:17:14.160] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002506)  
Hindi naman po ito US exclusive initiative.

**[03:17:17.680] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002507)  
Dahil po as of June 2026, meron na pong 24 signatories.

**[03:17:23.420] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002508)  
Ang Paxilica Declaration.

**[03:17:26.420] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002509)  
Kasama ang Pilipinas, United States, Australia, Japan, Republic of Korea, Singapore, the Netherlands, United Kingdom, India, Germany, Israel, United Arab Emirates, and other partner economies pa po.

**[03:17:42.800] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002510)  
So, ina-expect po natin na iba-iba po yung mga bansa na pwedeng mag-locate sa Clark Advanced Manufacturing Park.

**[03:17:53.420] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002511)  
Ilan naman po kaya yung tech firm?

**[03:17:58.180] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002512)  
Tech firm.

**[03:17:59.860] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002513)  
Wala pa po tayong final reply dyan kasi po nothing is final po as of the moment.

**[03:18:06.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002514)  
Salamat po, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[03:18:09.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002515)  
Dadako po ako sa aking pangatlong katanungan.

**[03:18:13.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002516)  
Ano po kaya ang specific roles ng BCDA sa Paxilica at sa Clark Advanced Manufacturing Park?

**[03:18:23.420] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002517)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker. Ang BCDA po ay isang IPA or Investments Promotions Agency.

**[03:18:30.960] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002518)  
So, yung role po niya ay limitado po sa pagiging isang commercial partner or lessor po ng CAMP or ng Clark Advanced Manufacturing Park.

**[03:18:43.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002519)  
Ilang staff po ba meron ang BCDA?

**[03:18:47.680] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002520)  
Ang BCDA po sa kabuan ay meron pong 257 na staff.

**[03:18:53.420] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002521)  
57?

**[03:18:55.260] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002522)  
257.

**[03:18:56.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002523)  
257. Salamat po.

**[03:18:58.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002524)  
Ilang staff naman po ang currently assigned sa Clark Advanced Manufacturing Park?

**[03:19:04.980] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002525)  
Sa ngayon po, Madam Speaker, wala pa pong dedicated staff para po sa ating Clark Advanced Manufacturing Park kasi wala pa naman pong concrete project and wala pang concrete roles.

**[03:19:17.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002526)  
Madam Sponsor, can the BCDA confirm that the agency?

**[03:19:23.420] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002527)  
Yes, Madam Speaker. Sa lahat naman po ng proyekto ng BCDA, nagbibigay po ng due proper notice ang BCDA po.

**[03:19:40.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002528)  
Bilang lead agency or government agency sa implementation ng Paxilica, paano isisiguro ng BCDA na compliant po tayo sa labor laws,

**[03:19:51.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002529)  
ang mga papasok na tech firms?

**[03:19:55.660] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002530)  
Madam Speaker, yung ating pong Clark Advanced Manufacturing Park ay ang masusunod po ay ang mga batas ng Pilipinas kasama po ang labor laws na i-implement po dito.

**[03:20:08.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002531)  
Ibig sabihin po, tayo po ang masusunod?

**[03:20:11.940] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002532)  
Yes po, Madam Speaker.

**[03:20:13.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002533)  
Gandang balita po yun kung tayo yung nasusunod at hindi po tayo minamandohan ng ibang bansa or ng foreign.

**[03:20:21.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002534)  
Saan sa Paxilica agreement nakasaad itong labor law compliance po?

**[03:20:29.760] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002535)  
Sa ngayon po, wala pa po tayong agreement. So, yung ating pong sinusunod sa lahat po ng proyekto ng BCDA ay ang ating pong Philippine labor laws.

**[03:20:41.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002536)  
So, ibig sabihin, wala pang provision or actual provision para dito. Pero yun yung plano.

**[03:20:48.120] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002537)  
Wala pa pong agreement, Madam Speaker.

**[03:20:51.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002538)  
Kung may kumpanyang non-compliant sa labor laws within the Clark Advanced Manufacturing Park, may kapangyarihan ba ang BCDA palayasin o pagbayarin sila?

**[03:21:05.260] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002539)  
Again, Madam Speaker, sinisigurado po ng BCDA na Philippine labor laws will apply. Pero sa ngayon po, wala pa pong existing agreement. But this is what we can assure you po.

**[03:21:17.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002540)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Sponsor.

**[03:21:19.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002541)  
Sana po ito po.

**[03:21:21.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002542)  
Kaya, magawa po talaga natin. Kasi ilang foreign countries na rin po ang pumasok sa ating bansa. Kagaya na lamang nung nakaraan, nagkaroon po ng labor strike ang ating mga DHL workers na kung saan sinasabi nilang labor laws ng Pilipinas ang sinusunod.

**[03:21:46.740] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002543)  
However, nung nagkaroon na ng union sa loob ng DHL.

**[03:21:51.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002544)  
Eh, bigla na lamang silang in-end yung contract ng mga workers.

**[03:21:57.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002545)  
So, sana po, hindi lamang po ito pangako ng Paxilica, kundi talagang magkakaroon ng provision at talagang mapanagot po natin yung kumpanya na lalabag dito.

**[03:22:10.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002546)  
With that, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, ang susunod po na katanungan ko, follow up lamang po.

**[03:22:18.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002547)  
Saan sa Paxilica agreement naman?

**[03:22:21.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002548)  
Saan po, nakasaad na may kapangyarihan ang BCDA na i-penalize ang mga non-compliant companies, kung meron man po?

**[03:22:29.940] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002549)  
Again, Madam Speaker, wala pa pong agreement. Pero again, sinusigurado po ng BCDA na magiging compliant po tayo sa mga batas ng Pilipinas.

**[03:22:42.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002550)  
Sana po, kung ano yung napag-usapan natin dito, uulitin ko po, kung sinasabi ninyo ganyan,

**[03:22:50.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002551)  
maganda po yung balita. Pero hanggat wala pong agreement, wala pong provision, wala pong papel, nakakabahala pa rin po.

**[03:22:59.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002552)  
Susunod po na katanungan ko ay, paano naman po isisiguro ng BCDA na malayang makapag-unionize ang mga manggagawa sa loob ng mga development projects na ito?

**[03:23:13.880] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002553)  
Again, Madam Speaker, we will follow Philippine labor laws.

**[03:23:17.680] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002554)  
At ito naman po ay nakasaad sa provision po natin.

**[03:23:21.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002555)  
Kaugnay po nung nangyari sa DHL, sana hindi po mangyari yung nangyari sa DHL na Philippine labor law ang sinunod,

**[03:23:31.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002556)  
pero nung nagka-unionize, biglang umayaw na si DHL. So sana hindi po gano'n yung mangyayari dito.

**[03:23:40.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002557)  
Paano naman po isisiguro ng BCDA na ang mga manggagawang Pilipino,

**[03:23:46.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002558)  
ang majority na mahahire sa mga high quality jobs sa Clark Advanced Manufacturing Park?

**[03:23:53.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002559)  
Baka naman po kasi dahil foreign country yung papasok dito,

**[03:23:59.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002560)  
baka naman po dadalhin din nila yung kanilang mga manggagawa mula sa ibang bansa.

**[03:24:16.020] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-002561)  
Ayon po sa ating BOI, ang nakasaad po ay minimum 90% po ang mga Pilipino na magtatrabaho.

**[03:24:31.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002562)  
As a-handing po natin yan na makikita natin sa provision or sa agreement po.

**[03:24:38.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002563)  
At hindi ko na itatanong kung saan parte po yun ng agreement ng Paxilica dahil kagaya ng nasagot nyo po kanina,

**[03:24:46.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-002564)

**[05:00:19.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004479)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor, good evening.

**[05:00:24.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004480)  
And will the sponsor be open to answer a few questions in relation to the budget

**[05:00:33.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004481)  
and the policies of Subic Bay Metropolitan Authority?

**[05:00:37.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004482)  
Yes, gladly, Madam Speaker.

**[05:00:39.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004483)  
Okay. Madam Speaker, good sponsor, we note that under the watch of the current administration,

**[05:00:47.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004484)  
SBMA has allowed 234 containers of suspected e-waste from the United States

**[05:00:53.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004485)  
to enter Subic Bay.

**[05:00:54.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004486)  
Since March 2025.

**[05:00:57.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004487)  
And another 184 containers almost arrived between August and September 2026,

**[05:01:04.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004488)  
all without the prior informed consent required by the Basel Convention.

**[05:01:08.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004489)  
Sa ilalim po ng Basel Convention, Madam Speaker, good sponsor,

**[05:01:12.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004490)  
ng e-waste amendment na pumasok sa bisa noong Enero 2025,

**[05:01:17.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004491)  
halos lahat ng e-waste, hazardous man o hindi, ay kontrolado ng konbensyon.

**[05:01:24.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004493)  
Malinaw sa probisyon ng Article 4, subsection 5,

**[05:01:28.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004494)  
nagbabawal sa kalakalan ng basura sa isang non-party tulad ng United States.

**[05:01:35.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004495)  
In fact, kung meron pong trade or export galing OECD countries, EU countries,

**[05:01:41.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004496)  
or Liechtenstein, dapat limited lang sa kanila at hindi po sa labas nito mangyari.

**[05:01:48.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004497)  
Nonetheless, there's been export of e-waste from the United States imported to the Philippines.

**[05:01:54.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004498)  
Wala rin pong anumang bilateral agreement na nagpapahintulot ng importasyon ng e-waste mula sa US papunta sa Pilipinas.

**[05:02:02.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004499)  
This is confirmed by the Department of Foreign Affairs.

**[05:02:05.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004500)  
Ang umiira lamang nakasunduan ay para sa exports ng ating bansa papunta sa US, and not the other way around.

**[05:02:13.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004501)  
We have heard preliminarily some defenses of SBMA,

**[05:02:18.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004502)  
and this is also what is provided in an RTC ruling,

**[05:02:23.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004503)  
which allowed...

**[05:02:24.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004504)  
the activities of such trade in SBMA.

**[05:02:29.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004505)  
But Madam Speaker, Good Sponsor, my question is,

**[05:02:33.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004506)  
does being a separate customs territory as provided in law enable SBMA to circumvent,

**[05:02:40.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004507)  
or essentially not follow, ratified international treaties and agreements?

**[05:02:54.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004508)  
Madam Speaker, the SBMA confirms the importation of scrapped mixed metals, computer accessories, used meter, used electronics, and scrapped copper.

**[05:03:08.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004509)  
However, these importations, as per the SBMA, adhere to international treaties.

**[05:03:15.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004510)  
Madam Speaker.

**[05:03:16.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004511)  
Madam Speaker, Good Sponsor, ano pong reaction ng SBMA na...

**[05:03:22.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004512)  
na under the Convention, and even under the implementing DNR Department Order,

**[05:03:31.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004513)  
wala naman pong pagbubukod ng e-waste, na ito po ay separate or hindi considered as hazardous wastes.

**[05:03:42.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004514)  
Hazardous wastes po siya na nililimitahan yung import-export regulation trade po nito ng Basel Convention,

**[05:03:51.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004515)  
which...

**[05:03:52.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004516)  
which explicitly provides, hindi po pwedeng magkaroon ng export from a non-party to a state party.

**[05:04:10.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004517)  
...

**[05:04:21.080] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-004518)  
What does the

**[05:04:21.920] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-004519)  
sponsor say?

**[05:04:28.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004520)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:04:32.320] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004521)  
SBMA still confirms that all importations of scrapped mixed metals are not hazardous.

**[05:04:41.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004522)  
These are...

**[05:04:44.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004523)  
These all adhere to all environmental regulations.

**[05:04:52.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004524)  
Madam Speaker, Good Sponsor, the Basel Action Network, which maintains and checks the compliance of states with the Basel Convention,

**[05:05:06.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004525)  
found in its field investigations in both the U.S. and the Philippines, that these containers that arrived at the Subic Port from the United States,

**[05:05:18.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004526)  
both contain hazardous electronic waste, classified as...

**[05:05:22.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004527)  
A-1181, and e-waste, requiring special consideration or Code 949.

**[05:05:28.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004528)  
These are Basel waste listings, illegal to be imported into the Philippines from the U.S.

**[05:05:35.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004529)  
due to the fact, again, that the U.S. is a non-party to the Convention.

**[05:05:38.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004530)  
Madam Speaker, Good Sponsor, what is the explanation behind the assertion that there is following of international treaty,

**[05:05:51.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004531)  
given the...

**[05:05:52.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004532)  
the citation of this representation right now?

**[05:05:57.560] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004533)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:05:59.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004534)  
the SBMA is willing to cooperate and to also submit to our Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:06:06.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004535)  
supporting documents that say that these materials are not hazardous,

**[05:06:15.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004536)  
just to establish that it also complies with all environmental compliances,

**[05:06:20.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004537)  
as well as international.

**[05:06:22.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004538)  
Madam Speaker, we will welcome these submissions.

**[05:06:26.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004539)  
But I'd like an explanation on the floor right now.

**[05:06:31.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004540)  
Ano po yung basihan kung bakit nasasabi, kahit succinctly po, Madam Speaker, Good Sponsor,

**[05:06:38.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004541)  
bakit po hindi iligal yung kalakala na ito?

**[05:06:41.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004542)  
Ano ang basihan kung bakit hindi po iligal ito?

**[05:06:45.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004543)  
We have heard the RTC ruling, which says nga po na separate customs territory ang SBMA.

**[05:06:51.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004544)  
But I believe...

**[05:06:52.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004545)  
Madam Speaker, Good Sponsor,

**[05:06:53.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004546)  
that doesn't mean that they are exempted from the Basel Convention,

**[05:06:56.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004547)  
the DNR Department Order implementing it,

**[05:07:00.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004548)  
and even other prevailing international laws and domestic regulations.

**[05:07:06.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004549)  
So, Madam Speaker, we will look forward to that.

**[05:07:09.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004550)  
But ano po on the floor, succinctly, ang rason?

**[05:07:13.020] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004551)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, just for the record also,

**[05:07:16.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004552)  
it's because the materials are not hazardous waste.

**[05:07:20.740] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004553)  
So, if I may...

**[05:07:22.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004554)  
Again, repeat.

**[05:07:24.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004555)  
These importations are scrap mixed metals, computer accessories,

**[05:07:30.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004556)  
used meters, used electronics, and scrap copper, which are not hazardous.

**[05:07:36.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004557)  
So, SBMA reiterates that these are not hazardous waste.

**[05:07:42.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004558)  
And so, the importation of such materials are not violated,

**[05:07:47.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004559)  
or doesn't violate international treaties and the Basel Convention as well.

**[05:07:52.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004560)  
Under what legal basis can the SBMA provide now to say that they are not hazardous waste?

**[05:08:01.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004561)  
Is this internal assessment?

**[05:08:03.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004562)  
Is this a different citation which they can state where it is classified differently

**[05:08:13.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004563)  
because the prevailing rules as of now lists the containers as hazardous electronic wastes and e-waste,

**[05:08:22.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004564)  
is requiring special consideration?

**[05:08:32.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004565)  
Madam Speaker, as for the SBMA, these materials are all inspected from importation.

**[05:08:44.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004566)  
Inspected by whom, Madam Speaker? Good sponsor.

**[05:08:46.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004567)  
Sorry.

**[05:08:47.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004568)  
Inspected by whom?

**[05:08:49.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004569)  
By the SBMA, Madam Speaker.

**[05:08:51.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004570)  
Is the...

**[05:08:51.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004571)  
The Bureau of Customs and DNR involved in the inspections, Madam Speaker. Good sponsor.

**[05:08:56.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004572)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[05:08:57.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004573)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[05:08:58.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004574)  
For now, there is no legal basis that has been cited other than the assurance that SBMA inspected them.

**[05:09:10.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004575)  
So, Madam Speaker, good sponsor, given the limited time that I have,

**[05:09:15.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004576)  
let it be stated on the record that to the question and to the...

**[05:09:21.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004577)  
legal basis that this representation cited, there has been no counter-legal basis provided.

**[05:09:27.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004578)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor, my next line of question would be regarding the position of SBMA

**[05:09:35.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004579)  
in relation to a Committee on Ecology meeting I had the opportunity to attend on March 11, 2026,

**[05:09:44.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004580)  
where our topic is hazardous waste management and strengthening the Environmental Management Bureau.

**[05:09:51.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004581)  
We talked about the possible amendment of the Toxic and Hazardous Wastes Act present and prevailing in our jurisprudence now.

**[05:10:02.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004582)  
SBMA was in attendance and it gave the position that they are pushing for the designation of free trade zones such as SUBIC

**[05:10:12.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004583)  
to become Basel Convention Free Zones.

**[05:10:16.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004584)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor, ano pong ibig sabihin ng SBMA nung sinabi niya na gustong maging Basel Convention Free Zone

**[05:10:22.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004587)  
ang Subic Freeport?

**[05:10:25.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004588)  
And is the agency asking for an exception from international treaties and our local environmental laws?

**[05:10:43.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004589)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, the reason cited by the SBMA is some locators would want to

**[05:10:51.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004591)  
expand their operations to include imports from the United States, and because the United States is not a signatory of the Basel Convention, that is the basis for the statement of the SBMA.

**[05:11:06.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004592)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor, I think desiring trade with the United States is not a heavy enough justification to look for an exception from a prevailing international treaty that seeks to protect the Philippines from the transboundary waste trade disproportionately affecting Southeast Asian countries, to the point that there has to be a call from Southeast Asian countries that [unclear] not the

**[05:11:51.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004593)  
dumping ground or trash can of developed countries. But at the end of the day, it will remove us from protections already in place, not just in discussion with the US but also with other countries that may seek to do trade.

**[05:12:21.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004594)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor, we will ask instead for a written explanation for that comment given in the March 11, 2026 hearing.

**[05:12:30.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004595)  
My next question, Madam Speaker, good sponsor, magkano po ba ang kinikita ng SBMA mula sa kalakalan ng recyclable waste?

**[05:12:41.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004596)

**[05:12:45.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004597)

**[05:13:19.300] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-004598)

**[05:13:50.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004600)

**[05:14:05.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004601)  
205,864,939 pesos for 2025.

**[05:14:12.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004602)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor, in the interest of time, we will also be requesting the same to be submitted via a written report.

**[05:14:21.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004604)

**[05:14:32.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004605)  
My next question would be in relation to its budget.

**[05:14:36.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004606)

**[05:15:00.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004609)

**[05:15:24.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004613)

**[05:15:25.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004614)  
My next question would be in relation to the joint efforts of the BOC, the DENREMB, and SBMA,

**[05:15:32.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004615)

**[05:15:39.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004617)

**[05:15:43.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004618)  
Can this representation be furnished with a copy of this report, Madam Speaker, Good Sponsor?

**[05:15:48.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004619)  
Yes, we will comply, Madam Speaker.

**[05:15:50.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004620)

**[05:15:54.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004621)  
ilan ang...

**[05:15:55.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004622)  
di lang ng mga nag-ooperate ng mga recycling locators doon

**[05:15:59.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004623)  
because when we asked DENREMB yesterday, wala raw po silang data on the recycling facilities in Subic.

**[05:16:07.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004624)

**[05:16:13.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004625)  
SBMA, Madam Speaker, Good Sponsor.

**[05:16:15.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004626)

**[05:16:28.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004633)  
And my last line of questioning, Madam Speaker, this would be in relation to the public pronouncements of SBMA

**[05:16:36.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004634)

**[05:17:17.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004640)

**[05:17:25.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004642)

**[05:17:32.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004644)

**[05:17:55.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004651)

...

**[05:18:43.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004662)  
Ano po ba ang ambag ng SBMA sa ekonomiya ng Pilipinas?

**[05:18:50.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004663)

**[05:18:52.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004664)

**[05:18:55.380] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004666)  
Madam Speaker, briefly, as of now, there are 1,783 locators.

Hektars.

**[05:20:37.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004702)  
Sixty-seven thousand hektars.

**[05:20:39.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004703)  
Sixty-seven thousand hektars.

Okay.

Container throughput.

Yung container, shipping container.

Sabi po rito, latest figure 2024.

**[05:21:15.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004718)  
Roughly 600,000 TEU.

**[05:21:19.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004719)

**[05:21:25.220] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004720)  
Yes.

Is that more or less correct?

**[05:21:28.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004722)

**[05:21:31.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004723)  
Yes, total capacity is 600,000, Madam Speaker.

**[05:21:34.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004724)  
Okay.

**[05:21:35.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004725)  
At in terms of GDP, meron ba kayong figure sa ambag ng subik sa GDP, kumbaga, ng Pilipinas?

**[05:21:50.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004726)  
Meron lang po tayo for, ano, well, overall contribution to the national economy.

**[05:21:55.220] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004727)  
So, for 2020, isa sabi dito po yung collections from the Bureau of Customs, yung mga taxes po na binabayaran sa BIR, yung dividends po na babayaran sa national government, tapos sa LGU share.

**[05:22:12.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004728)  
So, 2025 po, 53.1 billion pesos.

**[05:22:19.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004729)  
Consistent po na over 50 billion since 2022, per year, na nabibigay po ng SBMA.

**[05:22:24.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004730)  
Consistent po na.

**[05:22:25.220] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004731)  
Over 50 billion po ang naibibigay ng SBMA.

**[05:22:28.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004732)  
Consistently, over 50 billion ang nabibigay ng subik ng SBMA. Tama po ba?

**[05:22:34.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004733)  
Apo.

**[05:22:34.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004734)  
Sa national government. So, safe to say, napakahalaga po nito, hindi lang sa regional economy ng Region 3, kundi national economy. Tama po ba?

**[05:22:44.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004735)  
Apo, Madam Speaker.

**[05:22:45.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004736)  
Tama. Okay.

**[05:22:47.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004737)  
At kanina, pinanong natin sa BCDA,

**[05:22:53.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004738)  
kinonfirm nila.

**[05:22:53.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004739)  
Gusto ko rin marinig sa inyo, nagkaroon po ng MOU sa pagitan ng BCDA at SBMA designating the port of subik as the primary entry and exit point for logistics, etc. under Paxilica. Correct?

**[05:23:09.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004740)  
Tama po ba yun?

**[05:23:10.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004741)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[05:23:11.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004742)  
Okay. So, in other words, involved din po sa Paxilica ang subik. Tama po?

**[05:23:18.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004743)  
Yes, Madam Speaker. As a port.

**[05:23:21.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004744)  
As the port. Parang dito yung main na...

**[05:23:23.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004745)  
Entry and exit point. Dalu yan ng mga investment, produkto, and so on. Including, for example, energy. Parang ganun po yung narinig natin.

**[05:23:35.420] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004746)  
Madam Speaker, as per the MOU, binasignate lang po ang port of subik as yung gateway for the maritime logistics requirements.

**[05:23:45.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004747)  
Okay. Sige. Okay. Now, yung subik, hindi po yan EDCA site, hindi po ba?

**[05:23:53.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004748)  
Hindi po, Madam Speaker.

**[05:23:57.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004749)  
And necessarily, hindi siya EDCA site kasi yung mga approved locations under the EDCA agreement, they have to be properties provided by the AFP and DND, mga military locations. So, subik, hindi. Tama?

**[05:24:12.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004750)  
Hindi po, Madam Speaker.

**[05:24:12.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004751)  
Nevertheless, tama rin po ba na malaganap ang gamit ng US military sa subik? Is that correct?

**[05:24:23.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004752)  
Yes.

**[05:24:25.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004753)  
Okay.

**[05:24:39.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004754)  
Naku, yung oras tumutuloy, Madam Speaker. So, i-consider nyo na lang yan.

**[05:24:43.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-004755)  
If our distinguished colleague is amenable, the SBMA family can actually furnish your office to our good sponsor, a written response to your queries.

**[05:24:53.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004756)  
Okay. That would be great, Madam Speaker. But I think the sponsor is also ready to give a response.

**[05:25:00.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004757)  
Yes. The SBMA confirms that there are economic activities that involve American firms, American actors.

**[05:25:10.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004758)  
American firms servicing the US military. Correct?

**[05:25:14.620] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004759)  
Yes. Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[05:25:15.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004760)  
For example, let me cite, ito based on mga open source research, diba? Subik Dry Dock Corporation. Tama po ba?

**[05:25:23.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004761)  
Yes.

**[05:25:24.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004762)  
Ginagawa nila, repair and overhaul of US Navy vessels. Correct?

**[05:25:32.560] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004763)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[05:25:33.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004764)  
Okay. The PCSPC fuel terminal, yung pinakamalaking fuel depot sa buong bansa, in fact, that is owned by an American equity firm, I-squared Capital.

**[05:25:48.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004765)  
No?

**[05:25:49.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004766)  
At, hindi po ba, among others, it stores fuel...

**[05:25:53.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004767)  
It stores fuel for exclusive use of the US Navy. Tama po ba? Among others, hindi lahat. Hindi lahat. Pero merong fuel na ginagamit ng US Navy.

**[05:26:06.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004768)  
Non-exclusive, Madam Speaker.

**[05:26:07.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004769)  
Not exclusive. Pero meron. Tama po. Correct?

**[05:26:11.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004770)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[05:26:12.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004771)  
Okay. Alam ko, yung recently, yung PNOC, bumili ng diesel, tapos nilagay din dyan. Tama po ba?

**[05:26:19.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004772)  
Correct.

**[05:26:23.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004773)  
Strategic Oil Reserve or Fuel Reserve ay nandyan, katabi nung fuel ng US Navy. Tama po?

**[05:26:34.560] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004774)  
Opo.

**[05:26:34.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004775)  
Tama. Okay.

**[05:26:37.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004776)  
February 2025, US Marine Corps Repositioning Warehouse. This is a 5,300 square meter facility used to store vehicle communications assets, engineering assets. Correct?

**[05:26:53.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004777)  
Yes, we confirm, Madam Speaker.

**[05:26:56.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004778)  
Correct. So yung US Marine Corps, meron silang storage facility dyan.

**[05:27:04.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004779)  
Army staging. Aguila, Subic. Processing and storage of US Army equipment.

**[05:27:13.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004780)  
Tama po ba? Ito, yung Aguila facility sa Subic, ginagamit ng US Army naman ito.

**[05:27:23.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004781)  
Isa, Marine Corps. Ito naman, Army.

**[05:27:27.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004782)  
And the contractor for that is an American logistics contractor for the U.S. military known as V2X.

**[05:27:38.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004783)  
Yun po, V2X. Alam po niyan ni Chairman.

**[05:27:42.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004784)  
Okay. V2X is a defense contractor for logistics ng U.S. military.

**[05:27:49.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004785)  
So ginagamit nila yan.

**[05:27:50.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004786)  
Yun, part of Subic Bay, ginagamit routinely for maintenance ng mga U.S. Navy ships including submarines.

**[05:28:07.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004787)  
Diba, yung mga Ohio-class nuclear submarines ng U.S. ay nandyan.

**[05:28:13.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004788)  
Dumadaong dyan, nagpapaayos, nagpaparefuel, and so on and so forth.

**[05:28:18.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004789)  
The most recent incident being a U.S. destroyer that was recently stranded for four days in the South China Sea.

**[05:28:37.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004790)  
Tapos nawala ng kuryente. Tapos dinala sa Subic. Tama po ba?

**[05:28:45.580] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004791)  
Yes, we confirm, Madam Speaker.

**[05:28:47.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004792)  
Correct?

**[05:28:48.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004793)  
Yes, we confirm, Madam Speaker.

**[05:28:50.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004794)  
You confirm, no? Okay.

**[05:28:53.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004795)  
So, malinaw po, as I said, extensively used by the U.S. military ang Subic.

**[05:28:59.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004796)  
In fact, mas ginagamit pa po ito ng U.S. military kesa sa mga EDCA locations, yung approved locations.

**[05:29:08.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004797)  
Tama po ba?

**[05:29:10.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004798)  
O hindi n?yo na, hindi n?yo na...

**[05:29:12.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004799)  
Hindi na sa inyo manggagaling. Ako na nagsasabi na ito talaga yung main...

**[05:29:18.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004800)  
Entry and exit point, storage point, logistics hub ng U.S. military dito sa Pilipinas.

**[05:29:26.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004801)  
Lalo na pagbalikatan. At saka yung ibang mga exercises. So, napakalinaw po nun.

**[05:29:30.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004802)  
Okay. At the same time, sinabi natin kanina, this is the main economic engine in Luzon.

**[05:29:41.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004803)  
And in much of... and in the larger Filipino economy, correct?

**[05:29:46.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004804)  
Yes.

**[05:29:46.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004805)  
Yes. Okay.

**[05:29:47.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004806)  
Okay. Ngayon, ito po ang isang concern natin dito, no?

**[05:29:54.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004807)  
Ano po ang nangyari sa Iran war, particularly sa Gulf State?

**[05:30:00.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004808)  
Diba?

**[05:30:03.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004809)  
Pwede po, meron tayong visual na naman yata.

**[05:30:09.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004810)  
Well, it is well known that during this ongoing, di pa po tapos na gera sa Iran,

**[05:30:18.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004811)  
of course, sinalakay ng US ang Iran way back end of February, beginning of March,

**[05:30:27.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004812)  
hanggang ngayon nagpapatuloy, nagdulot ng krisis na energy crisis.

**[05:30:33.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004813)  
We are still under a state of energy emergency, di ba?

**[05:30:37.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004814)  
Because of the closure of the Strait of Hormuz.

**[05:30:40.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004815)  
Pero ang isang nangyari po ay tinarget, in response, tinarget ng Iran

**[05:30:47.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004816)  
using their missile and drone capability, advanced missile and drone capability,

**[05:30:55.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004817)  
drone and missile capability.

**[05:30:57.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004818)  
Una, US bases throughout the Gulf.

**[05:31:02.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004819)  
In fact, nawasak yung main naval base ng 5th Fleet ng US sa Bahrain.

**[05:31:11.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004820)  
Kaya nga namu-problema ang US Navy ngayon dahil wala na silang main base logistics hub and so on in the Gulf.

**[05:31:22.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004821)  
Okay, wasak po yung base, umatras ang US na palayas, in other words, ng Iran.

**[05:31:27.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004822)  
Sa kanilang mga base militar, ang US.

**[05:31:31.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004823)  
Pero hindi lang po military targets ang tinarget ng Iran.

**[05:31:36.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004824)  
Tinarget din po nila extensively ang mga dual-use targets.

**[05:31:44.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004825)  
In other words, civilian facilities na may ginagamit din ng US military.

**[05:31:48.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004826)  
So, for example,

**[05:31:50.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004828)  
um,

**[05:31:53.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004830)  
according to our research,

**[05:31:58.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004831)  
the Iran,

**[05:31:59.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004832)  
Iran,

**[05:32:00.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004833)  
and its Iran-aligned groups,

**[05:32:04.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004834)  
for example, yung the Houthis in Yemen,

**[05:32:07.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004835)  
conducted at least 172 attacks on non-military infrastructure

**[05:32:12.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004836)  
across the six Gulf Cooperation Council states.

**[05:32:17.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004837)  
So, 172 attacks.

**[05:32:19.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004838)  
Anong mga tinarget?

**[05:32:21.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004839)  
Energy facilities.

**[05:32:22.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004841)  
Oil, gas, power, desalination facilities, airports and ports, yung Kuwait Airport, ay tinarget yan kasi ginagamit ng US military.

**[05:32:34.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004842)  
Commercial, mga industrial facilities, financial facilities, government facilities, data centers, for example.

**[05:32:42.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004843)  
Ito yung isang recent news report, sabi dito, ay Iran strikes on Amazon data centers.

**[05:32:52.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004844)  
These are Amazon data centers in the United Arab Emirates mainly, no?

**[05:32:59.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004845)  
UAE and Bahrain.

**[05:33:03.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004846)  
So, tinarget din po.

**[05:33:04.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004847)  
Sabi ng Iran, basta ginagamit ng US, no?

**[05:33:09.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004848)  
Whether yung explicitly military or, in this case, data centers.

**[05:33:17.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004849)  
Kasi AI, ginagamit yan ng US military.

**[05:33:21.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004850)  
So tinarget din nila.

**[05:33:24.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004852)  
And they very precisely targeted the data centers of Amazon in Bahrain as well as in the UAE.

**[05:33:34.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004853)  
Dalawang areas, no?

**[05:33:36.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004854)  
And the damage was so extensive na, sabi rito, caused permanent data loss.

**[05:33:43.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004855)  
Na yung mga data center, yung mga malalaking korporasyon, they upload all their data on the cloud, di ba?

**[05:33:49.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004856)  
To Amazon.

**[05:33:52.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004857)  
Isa sa pinakalimutan.

**[05:33:52.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004858)  
Pinaka-trusted, pinaka-malaki na cloud service along with Google and Microsoft, yung Azure.

**[05:34:01.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004859)  
So, in the case of Amazon, nawasak yung data center sa Bahrain, nawasak din yung sa UAE, nawala na yung backup.

**[05:34:09.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004860)  
Even though, isa sa mga pinagmamalaki ng Amazon, redundancy, siyempre, di ba?

**[05:34:15.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004861)  
Pag nasira yung isa dyan, meron kang backup.

**[05:34:18.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004862)  
Dito po, wasak po lahat.

**[05:34:19.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004863)  
Wala ng backup.

**[05:34:20.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004864)  
So, that means...

**[05:34:22.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004865)  
Significant damage, not just to the infrastructure, business damage, di ba?

**[05:34:26.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004866)  
Nawala na yung data ng mga kliyente, nung Amazon.

**[05:34:32.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004867)  
So, yan ang sinasabi dyan sa artikulo na yan.

**[05:34:36.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004868)  
Okay, that's just one example.

**[05:34:39.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004869)  
Of course, other major attacks are on Kuwait, for example, yung kanilang natural gas facility.

**[05:34:49.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004870)  
Very precisely targeted.

**[05:34:51.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004871)  
Kaya nga po, matindihan.

**[05:34:52.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004872)  
Ngayon, ang problema sa supply ng natural gas, among others.

**[05:34:57.080] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-004873)

**[05:36:15.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004900)

**[05:36:17.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004901)  
Okay.

Anong point ko?

**[05:37:04.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004924)

**[05:38:33.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004972)

**[05:38:36.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004973)  
Okay, alam ito ng US.

sa SBMA,

ay,

against

ah,

military attack

**[05:38:56.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004986)

Under the SBMA,

ah,

under the

**[05:39:51.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005025)  
Well, Madam Speaker,

Second,

well, besides the fact

**[05:40:15.580] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005036)
