**[05:40:17.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005037)  
the SBMA also

**[05:40:18.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005038)  
continually coordinates

**[05:40:20.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005039)  
with local institutions

**[05:40:21.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005040)  
and law enforcement agencies.

**[05:40:23.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005041)  
Okay.

**[05:40:24.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005042)  
Kaya nga po,

**[05:40:24.660] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005043)  
ang daming maritime interdictions

**[05:40:27.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005044)  
na nahuhuli.

**[05:40:29.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005045)  
Not just the espionage activities,

**[05:40:33.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005046)  
but even anti-smuggling

**[05:40:35.380] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005047)  
and anti-drug operations.

**[05:40:37.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005048)  
Okay.

**[05:40:37.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005049)  
Oo.

**[05:40:37.740] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005050)  
So,

**[05:40:38.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005051)  
in terms of their capability

**[05:40:40.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005052)  
as to how to protect themselves

**[05:40:43.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005053)  
from attacks,

**[05:40:45.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005054)  
palagi naman pong coordinated

**[05:40:47.320] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005055)  
ang SBMA sa AFP,

**[05:40:50.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005056)  
sa iba't iba po po natin,

**[05:40:51.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005057)  
mga law enforcement agencies.

**[05:40:55.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005058)  
Besides that,

**[05:40:56.740] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005059)  
meron silang intelligence office,

**[05:40:59.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005060)  
so kasama dyan yung mga

**[05:41:01.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005061)  
different intelligence agencies natin.

**[05:41:04.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005062)  
Yun yung primary way

**[05:41:06.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005063)  
in which pinaprotektahan ng SBMA

**[05:41:08.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005064)  
yung sarili nila.

**[05:41:09.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005065)  
Madam Speaker.

**[05:41:10.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005066)  
Okay.

**[05:41:10.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005067)  
Sabi po sa Section 9

**[05:41:11.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005068)  
ng Special Economic Zone Law,

**[05:41:15.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005069)  
Ecozone Law,

**[05:41:16.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005070)  
Section 9,

**[05:41:16.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005071)  
Defense and Security,

**[05:41:18.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005072)  
the Defense of the Ecozone

**[05:41:19.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005073)  
and the Security of its Perimeter Fence,

**[05:41:21.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005074)  
shall be the responsibility

**[05:41:23.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005075)  
of the National Government

**[05:41:25.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005076)  
in coordination with the PESA.

**[05:41:28.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005077)  
So, essentially,

**[05:41:29.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005078)  
nakadepende talaga sa National Government.

**[05:41:32.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005079)  
Meron po bang

**[05:41:33.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005080)  
drone defenses

**[05:41:35.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005081)  
ang SBMA?

**[05:41:38.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005082)  
Kaya pong

**[05:41:39.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005083)  
protektahan sa drone?

**[05:41:42.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005084)  
Pag inatake ng drone,

**[05:41:44.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005085)  
may depensa po?

**[05:41:45.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005086)  
Ang SBMA po, Madam Speaker,

**[05:41:48.220] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005087)  
wala.

**[05:41:48.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005088)  
Pero,

**[05:41:48.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005089)  
nag-coordinate po yung SBMA

**[05:41:50.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005090)  
with the Philippine Navy.

**[05:41:51.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005091)  
Philippine Navy ang merong drone.

**[05:41:54.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005092)  
E, anti-missile defense,

**[05:41:55.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005093)  
meron po ba kaya na available

**[05:41:57.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005094)  
ang

**[05:41:58.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005095)  
ang

**[05:42:00.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005096)  
SBMA?

**[05:42:02.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005097)  
Para protektahan po yung fuel dito.

**[05:42:04.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005098)  
E, pagka pinasabog yan,

**[05:42:07.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005099)  
wala na tayong

**[05:42:08.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005100)  
reserbang langis.

**[05:42:11.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005101)  
Ang SBMA po ay wala,

**[05:42:13.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005102)  
but ang Philippine Navy po ay meron.

**[05:42:15.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005103)  
Meron ang Philippine Navy.

**[05:42:18.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005104)  
Meron po.

**[05:42:18.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005105)  
Meron ba talaga?

**[05:42:19.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005106)  
Do we have?

**[05:42:21.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005107)  
Do we have such defenses?

**[05:42:26.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005108)  
Pinag-iisipan ba

**[05:42:28.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005109)  
ng SBMA

**[05:42:29.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005110)  
ang senaryo na ito?

**[05:42:50.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005112)  
Pinag-iisipan po ba

**[05:42:51.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005113)  
ng SBMA.

**[05:42:53.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005114)  
In other words, ano po, kung sakali,

**[05:42:55.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005115)  
you know, scenario building,

**[05:42:58.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005116)  
kung sakali, magkaroon

**[05:42:59.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005117)  
nga ng gera between US

**[05:43:01.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005118)  
and China,

**[05:43:03.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005119)  
the US government itself

**[05:43:05.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005120)  
is planning

**[05:43:07.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005121)  
for

**[05:43:08.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005122)  
a war

**[05:43:11.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005123)  
with China in 2027.

**[05:43:13.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005124)  
Yun po ang nasa mga national security

**[05:43:15.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005125)  
documents ng US. Sabi nila, by

**[05:43:17.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005126)  
2027, ay

**[05:43:19.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005127)  
posibleng

**[05:43:20.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005128)  
militarily ay

**[05:43:25.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005129)  
isisikapin ang China

**[05:43:27.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005130)  
na ma-integrate na

**[05:43:29.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005131)  
ang Taiwan.

**[05:43:31.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005132)  
So, yung so-called

**[05:43:33.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005133)  
Taiwan contingency. So, sa ganung

**[05:43:35.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005134)  
scenario,

**[05:43:36.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005135)  
2027 po ang kanilang sinasabi,

**[05:43:39.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005136)  
sa ganung scenario,

**[05:43:42.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005137)  
at kung sakaling mawasak

**[05:43:43.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005138)  
po ang mga pasilidad

**[05:43:45.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005139)  
na ginagamit ng US sa

**[05:43:47.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005140)  
Subic, yung

**[05:43:49.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005141)  
fuel depot,

**[05:43:50.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005142)  
yung mga dry dock,

**[05:43:51.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005143)  
yung

**[05:43:52.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005144)  
ship building facility,

**[05:43:56.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005145)  
yung mga possibly, kung may mga

**[05:43:59.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005146)  
data centers, yung mga warehouse,

**[05:44:02.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005147)  
and so on,

**[05:44:04.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005148)  
ano pong magiging

**[05:44:05.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005149)  
epekto nun?

**[05:44:11.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005150)  
Ano kaya magiging epekto nun sa

**[05:44:13.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005151)  
SBMA at sa larger economy?

**[05:44:17.060] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005152)  
Well, yung kita

**[05:44:17.900] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005153)  
po ng SBMA, definitely

**[05:44:19.900] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005154)  
bababa po yun kung nangyari po yun.

**[05:44:22.200] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005155)  
So, in turn, may negative

**[05:44:23.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005156)  
effect po yun sa...

**[05:44:25.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005157)  
It would have a major effect, katulad nung sinabi

**[05:44:27.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005158)  
niyo kanina, at least 50 billion

**[05:44:29.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005159)  
a year, ang contribution sa

**[05:44:31.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005160)  
ekonomiya.

**[05:44:33.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005161)  
Magkano po ang kailangan para ma

**[05:44:35.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005162)  
restore,

**[05:44:37.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005163)  
ma-rehabilitate kung sakaling ma-damage

**[05:44:40.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005164)  
ang mga pasilidad na to?

**[05:44:42.640] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-005165)  
With the indulgence of our

**[05:44:43.900] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-005166)  
distinguished colleague, please wrap up.

**[05:44:46.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005167)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, I still

**[05:44:48.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005168)  
have time.

**[05:44:49.900] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005169)  
Well, we have to quantify that

**[05:44:51.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005170)  
given na may

**[05:44:52.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005171)  
rebuilding of facilities

**[05:44:56.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005172)  
and what not, but definitely

**[05:44:57.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005173)  
a big...

**[05:44:58.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005174)  
It would be a big cost.

**[05:45:00.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005175)  
So, definitely, dapat pag-isipan kung paano didipensahan nito. Tama po ba?

**[05:45:07.200] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005176)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[05:45:08.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005177)  
At sa ngayon, meron na po bang plano ang SBMA? Kaugnay niyan? Paano didipensahan ang mga ito?

**[05:45:22.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-005178)  
Well, in terms of defense strategy po, the SBMA defers to the Department of National Defense.

**[05:45:27.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005179)  
Well, Madam Speaker, siguro yun lang po ang pinakapunto natin dito.

**[05:45:34.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005180)  
Ang SBMA, ang BCDA, at ang Malacanang mismo ay talagang pinipresenta ang sarili dito sa Paxilica Initiative.

**[05:45:46.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005181)  
Kanina narinig natin sa BCDA, nag-offer na, dalawang taon libre, walang renta sa lupa, 1,600 hectares.

**[05:45:55.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005182)  
Tinanggap na ng US.

**[05:45:57.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005183)  
Samantalang wala pa namang anumang konkretong commitment ng US.

**[05:46:02.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005184)  
Di ba?

**[05:46:03.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005185)  
We accept. Pero kami, wala. Nothing. Wala naman. Puro laway pa lang.

**[05:46:11.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005186)  
So, ganun po ka-atat na atat.

**[05:46:14.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005187)  
Ang ating, ang administrasyong ito, at ang SBMA, aminado sila na napakalaki ng presensya ng US military

**[05:46:26.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005188)  
sa napakahalagang

**[05:46:27.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005189)  
economic infrastructure na ito.

**[05:46:33.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005190)  
At ang sinasabi po natin, hindi na po hypothetical.

**[05:46:38.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005191)  
Kung sakaling magkaroon ng gera sa pagitan ng US at China, at ito ang pinag-ahandaan ng US,

**[05:46:46.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005192)  
kaya nga sila nagpapalaki ng presensya dyan sa Subic,

**[05:46:52.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005193)  
ay may epekto po yan sa SBMA.

**[05:46:56.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005195)  
No?

**[05:46:57.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005196)  
Kayo po ang, ay mawawasak.

**[05:46:59.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005197)  
Hindi na po hypothetical yun dahil ganun ang nangyari sa mga bansa sa Gulf.

**[05:47:08.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005198)  
No?

**[05:47:08.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005199)  
In the so-called Gulf states.

**[05:47:10.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005200)  
Kuwait, Bahrain, UAE, and so on.

**[05:47:13.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005201)  
No?

**[05:47:13.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005202)  
Ang mga yun may sariling kakayahan na depensa ng sarili and yet di kinaya.

**[05:47:20.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005203)  
Nawasak.

**[05:47:20.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005204)  
Di ba?

**[05:47:21.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005205)  
Katulad nung nakita natin kanina.

**[05:47:22.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005206)  
Paano pa kaya ang Pilipinas?

**[05:47:24.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005207)  
So, I understand.

**[05:47:26.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005208)  
I understand this is a policy question that goes beyond the SBMA.

**[05:47:31.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005209)  
National issue na po ito.

**[05:47:33.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005210)  
Pero ang punto po natin ay tumataya ang Marcos Jr. Administration sa Paxilica na ito.

**[05:47:42.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005211)  
Sabi, the whole Luzon Economic Corridor.

**[05:47:45.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005212)  
So, not just Subic, but also Clark, pati yung sa Batangas, at iba pa na involved sa corridor na yan.

**[05:47:54.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005213)  
Bataan.

**[05:47:54.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005214)  
Nakikita ko ang kasamahan natin from Bataan.

**[05:47:58.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005215)  
Damay po kayo dyan.

**[05:48:00.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005216)  
Damay tayong lahat dyan.

**[05:48:02.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005217)  
And if ever matuloy yung gera na pinag-aandaan ng US laban sa China,

**[05:48:12.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005218)  
ang nakataya po ay ang malaking bahagi ng ating ekonomiya.

**[05:48:16.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005219)  
So, kung sakaling mangyari yung nangyari sa Gulf states,

**[05:48:24.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005220)  
ay babalik po tayo sa parang, in effect, parang pinatubo.

**[05:48:29.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005221)  
Kung ano yung nangyari sa Subic at Clark nung panahon ng pinatubo.

**[05:48:37.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005222)  
O, ganun na po.

**[05:48:38.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005223)  
Sagutin ko na sa inyo, anong mangyari dyan?

**[05:48:40.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005224)  
Parang pinatubo.

**[05:48:42.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005225)  
So, that would set us back by practically two generations.

**[05:48:47.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005226)  
Kung sakaling mangyari po yun.

**[05:48:48.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005227)  
So, the point here, Madam Speaker, is really to question,

**[05:48:54.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005228)  
the wisdom of this administration in betting on the so-called Pax Silitka

**[05:49:04.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005229)  
na ang bantapo ay sa pinaka-siguridad ng buhay ng mga Pilipino

**[05:49:10.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005230)  
at sa ekonomiya mismo at pag-unlad ng Pilipinas.

**[05:49:17.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005231)  
Nang walang anumang pag-iisip at pag-aanda para sa depensa.

**[05:49:25.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005232)  
Di ba?

**[05:49:26.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005233)  
Kaya po, with that, Madam Speaker, tatapusin ko na po ang aking interpellation.

**[05:49:31.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005234)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:49:32.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005235)  
No to Pax Silica

**[05:49:34.780] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-005236)  
Do we hear the motion to terminate?

**[05:49:39.980] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-005237)  
Madam Speaker, on the part of the minority.

The Honorable Tino, please proceed.

**[05:59:27.240] Rep. Amben S. Amante** (asr-005374)

**[05:59:39.800] Rep. Amben S. Amante** (asr-005375)

**[05:59:49.880] Rep. Amben S. Amante** (asr-005376)

**[06:00:00.180] Rep. Amben S. Amante** (asr-005377)  
11,264 gigabytes RAM,

**[06:00:04.060] Rep. Amben S. Amante** (asr-005378)

ah,

**[06:07:21.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005478)  
a recent,

**[06:07:56.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005487)

**[06:08:13.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005489)

**[06:08:21.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005491)  
The title of this speech is the Digital Sovereignty Trap.

**[06:08:30.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005492)  
Ayan.

**[06:08:31.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005493)  
Ganito po, let me read into the record the words of the main architect of Paxilica.

**[06:08:39.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005494)

**[06:08:48.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005495)  
These days,

**[06:08:50.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005496)  
few words flatter a government like digital sovereignty.

**[06:08:55.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005497)  
It carries the music of independence, the dignity of self-rule,

**[06:09:00.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005498)

**[06:09:04.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005499)

**[06:09:14.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005500)  
No?

**[06:09:15.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005501)  
It is a growing number of governments,

**[06:09:18.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005502)

**[06:09:24.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005503)

**[06:09:28.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005504)

**[06:09:47.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005511)

**[06:09:48.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005512)

**[06:09:50.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005513)

**[06:09:51.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005514)

**[06:09:54.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005515)  
O.

**[06:10:03.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005519)  
your own AI stack.

**[06:10:05.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005520)  
What does he have to say about it?

**[06:10:07.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005521)  
Quote,

**[06:10:08.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005522)  
It is a seductive vision.

**[06:10:10.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005523)  
It is a seductive vision.

**[06:10:38.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005535)  
Ibig sabihin,

**[06:10:39.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005536)  
eh, mga iba't ibang bansa,

**[06:10:40.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005537)

No?

So, pinagtatawanan po ni Helberg

hibang kayo,

Oh.
